Follow-ups: `access_scope_with` for upstream/route create/get/list/update/delete ([`routes/upstream.rs`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/routes/upstream.rs), [`routes/route.rs`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/routes/route.rs); actions per [`DESIGN.md:758-764`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L758-L764)), mirroring `gears/file-storage`. Add e2e for scenario `auth/negative-1.2`.

### M-02: The plugin management API (`/plugins`) does not exist, but the docs describe it and tick its entity and repository

- **Decision:** `FUTURE` + `FIX-DOC`, no priority. The plugin CRUD API, the Plugin entity and repository, and Starlark plugins are planned; mark them planned and untick what was ticked.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`DESIGN.md:629-635`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L629-L635)
    > | `POST` | `/api/oagw/v1/plugins` | Create plugin | … Plugins are immutable (no PUT). DELETE returns `409 PluginInUse` when referenced.
  - More DESIGN sections on custom plugins: [l.401](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L401), [l.532-540](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L532-L540) (lifecycle and GC), [l.840-849](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L840-L849) (`oagw_plugin` table).
  - [`PRD.md:562`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L562)
    > - **Description**: CRUD operations for upstreams, routes, and plugins via `/api/oagw/v1/upstreams`, `/api/oagw/v1/routes`, `/api/oagw/v1/plugins` endpoints.
  - [`PRD.md:169`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L169) (§4.1 In Scope)
    > - CRUD management of upstreams, routes, and plugins via REST API
  - [`F0001:213-215`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0001-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-domain-foundation.md#L213-L215)
    > - [x] `p1` - **ID**: `cpt-cf-oagw-dod-domain-entities` … `Upstream`, `Route`, `Plugin`, `ServerConfig`, and `Endpoint`
  - [`F0001:287-289`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0001-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-domain-foundation.md#L287-L289)
    > - [x] `p1` - **ID**: `cpt-cf-oagw-dod-domain-layering` … Domain layer **MUST** define repository traits (`UpstreamRepository`, `RouteRepository`, `PluginRepository`).
  - [scenario 4.2](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/plugins/negative-4.2-plugin-immutability.md#L22)
    > - `404 Not Found` or `405 Method Not Allowed` (lock expected behavior).
  - [`INDEX.md:147-149`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L147-L149)
    > #### Create custom Starlark guard plugin … `POST /plugins` returns `201`.
  - Other plugin scenarios: [4.1](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/plugins/positive-4.1-create-custom-starlark-guard-plugin.md), [4.3](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/plugins/positive-4.3-delete-plugin-succeeds-only-unreferenced.md), [4.5-B](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/plugins/positive-4.5-plugin-resolution-supports-builtin-named-ids-custom-uuid.md), [4.7](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/plugins/positive-4.7-plugin-usage-tracking-gc-eligibility-behavior.md), [1.2](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/auth/negative-1.2-permission-gates-upstream-route-plugin-crud.md); INDEX [l.145-165](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L145-L165).
- **What the code does:**
  - [`routes/mod.rs:34-38`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/routes/mod.rs#L34-L38)

    ```rust
        router = upstream::register(router, openapi, writable);
        router = route::register(router, openapi, writable);
        router = proxy::register(router);
    ```

    Only upstream, route and proxy routes exist. There is no Plugin model, no `PluginRepository`, no Starlark dependency and no `oagw_plugin` table anywhere in the gear.
  - [`domain/error.rs:136-139`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/error.rs#L136-L139): `PluginNotFound` and `PluginInUse` exist and are mapped to REST errors, but no code constructs them.
  - Scenario 4.2 (PUT → 404/405) "passes" only because the endpoint does not exist.
- **Evidence of intent:** planned. [`F0003:89`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0003-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-plugin-system.md#L89) “- [ ] `p1` - **ID**: `cpt-cf-oagw-flow-plugin-create`”, and most of `F0003` is `[ ]`. The sandbox is `p3` ([`F0003:391`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0003-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-plugin-system.md#L391), [`PRD.md:530`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L530)). The `F0001` DoD items were over-ticked by the [bulk checkbox sync](#why-the-docs-drifted).
- **Why this decision:** An unbuilt feature is marked planned, not built. PRD keeps plugin management as the target; its sections get a planned marker.
- **Impact:** Not breaking (no code change). Readers of PRD, DESIGN and INDEX learn that plugins cannot be managed through the API yet. Follow-ups: untick `F0001` DoD [l.213](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0001-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-domain-foundation.md#L213)/[l.287](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0001-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-domain-foundation.md#L287). Future-facing markers on scenarios 4.1, 4.2, 4.3, 4.5-B, 4.7, 1.2 and [`INDEX.md:145-165`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L145-L165). Mark DESIGN `/plugins` sections planned: [`DESIGN.md:401`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L401) “**Custom Plugins**: Starlark scripts with sandboxed execution”, [`DESIGN.md:532-540`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L532-L540) “#### Plugin Lifecycle Management”, [`DESIGN.md:629-635`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L629-L635), [`DESIGN.md:840-849`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L840-L849) “| `oagw_plugin` | Custom plugins (UUID-backed) |”; and PRD §4.1 [`PRD.md:169`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L169) and [`PRD.md:562`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L562). The unused `PluginNotFound`/`PluginInUse` variants stay for the planned feature ([PLG-03](#plg-03-an-unknown-plugin-id-gives-401-auth-500-guard-or-is-silently-skipped-transform-not-the-documented-503) now uses `plugin_not_found`).
- **Decided together with:** [PLG-03](#plg-03-an-unknown-plugin-id-gives-401-auth-500-guard-or-is-silently-skipped-transform-not-the-documented-503).

### M-03: A PUT that omits `tags` or `enabled` (or `priority` on routes) fails with 422, while docs promise 400 and show such bodies

- **Decision:** `FIX-DOC`, no priority. PUT is a full replacement: `tags` and `enabled` (and `priority` for routes) are required, and body-shape errors return 422. Docs and scenarios follow the code.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`F0002:94`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0002-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-management-api.md#L94)
    > - Validation fails (invalid endpoint format, missing required fields)
  - [`F0002:105`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0002-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-management-api.md#L105)
    > 1. [x] - `p1` - **RETURN** 400 ValidationError (RFC 9457 Problem Details) - `inst-create-us-6a`
  - [`DESIGN.md:647`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L647)
    > - Full replacement — all fields are overwritten; omitted optional fields are cleared.
  - [scenario 2.5](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/upstreams/positive-2.5-update-upstream.md#L39-L55): the PUT body sends `enabled` but no `tags`.

    ```json
      "protocol": "gts.cf.core.oagw.protocol.v1~cf.core.oagw.http.v1",
      "alias": "httpbin.org",
      "enabled": true,
      "headers": {
    ```

  - The PUT bodies in scenarios [2.6](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/upstreams/negative-2.6-disable-upstream-blocks-proxy-traffic.md#L15-L25), [2.11](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/upstreams/positive-2.11-re-enable-upstream-restores-proxy-traffic.md), [2.13](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/upstreams/positive-2.13-crud-endpoint-update-invalidates-load-balancer.md#L32-L40), [3.7](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/routes/negative-3.7-disable-route-blocks-proxy-traffic.md), [3.9](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/routes/positive-3.9-re-enable-route-restores-proxy-traffic.md), [4.4](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/plugins/negative-4.4-plugin-type-enforcement.md#L16-L28) and the [flow update example](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/flows/management-operations.md#L96-L107) also omit `tags` (and several omit `enabled`).
  - OpenAPI: [`docs/api/api.json:47757-47863`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/docs/api/api.json#L47757-L47863): the `PUT /oagw/v1/upstreams/{id}` operation lists 200, 400, 401, 403, 404, 409, 429 and 500, but not 422.
- **What the code does:**
  - [`dto.rs:357-358`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L357-L358)

    ```rust
        pub tags: Vec<String>,
        pub enabled: bool,
    ```

    The update DTO has no `#[serde(default)]` on these fields, unlike the create DTO ([`dto.rs:335-338`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L335-L338)). The route update DTO is the same for `tags`, `priority` and `enabled` ([`dto.rs:394-396`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L394-L396)).
  - [`management_api_test.rs:44-56`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/tests/management_api_test.rs#L44-L56): a body that fails to deserialize gets 422 (`create_upstream_missing_server_returns_422` asserts `.expect_status(422)`).
  - `id` and `upstream_id` sent in a PUT body are silently ignored (the update DTOs have no such fields, and unknown fields are not rejected).
- **Evidence of intent:** PATCH became full-replacement PUT in [`54bbb6706`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/54bbb67062f678668cdc34740727aaaa5a0f5d03) ("update PATCH requests to PUT to align with spec"); the scenarios predate it. The e2e helpers always send these fields ([`helpers.py:128-135`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/helpers.py#L128-L135), [`helpers.py:287-297`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/helpers.py#L287-L297)), which hides the problem.
- **Why this decision:** DESIGN states the full-replacement rule and the code implements it; scenarios and the feature doc rank lower and follow.
- **Impact:** Not breaking (the wire does not change). Small code touch: the utoipa annotations list 422. No `enabled` default: it would silently re-enable a disabled upstream (see [M-12](#m-12-a-disabled-upstream-silently-falls-through-to-an-ancestors-upstream-instead-of-returning-503)). Follow-ups: FIX-DOC [`F0002:94`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0002-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-management-api.md#L94)/[`105`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0002-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-management-api.md#L105), [`DESIGN.md:647`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L647), the scenario PUT bodies (2.5, 2.6, 2.11, 2.13, 3.7, 3.9, 4.4) and the [`flows/management-operations.md:96-107`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/flows/management-operations.md#L96-L107) example “"alias": "openai",”. Note that `id`/`upstream_id` in PUT bodies are ignored.
- **Decided together with:** [M-12](#m-12-a-disabled-upstream-silently-falls-through-to-an-ancestors-upstream-instead-of-returning-503).

### M-04: The upstream `protocol` field accepts any string and is never used

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE`, P2. Validate `protocol` at write time: 400 unless it is a registered protocol GTS id (only HTTP today).
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`F0001:169`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0001-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-domain-foundation.md#L169)
    > 2. [x] - `p1` - Validate `protocol` is a recognized GTS protocol identifier - `inst-val-2b`
  - [`F0002:300-301`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0002-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-management-api.md#L300-L301)
    > 6. [x] - `p1` - **IF** protocol not in [http, grpc] - `inst-val-us-6` … Add error: "Unsupported protocol: {protocol}"
  - [`upstream.v1.schema.json:68-73`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L68-L73)

    ```json
        "protocol": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "gts.cf.core.oagw.protocol.v1~cf.core.oagw.http.v1",
            "gts.cf.core.oagw.protocol.v1~cf.core.oagw.grpc.v1"
    ```

  - [`DESIGN.md:704`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L704)
    > Request classification uses `upstream.protocol` to determine match strategy:
  - [`ADR-0002:123`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0002-request-routing.md#L123)
    > At request time, the proxy handler resolves `{alias}` to an upstream first, then uses the upstream's `protocol` to determine which route match keys to apply:
  - [scenario 12.7](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/protocols/http/negative-12.7-scheme-protocol-mismatches-fail-explicitly.md#L3-L31)
    > Scenario A: protocol=http with scheme=grpc … Rejected at validation with `400` `application/problem+json`. … Scenario B: protocol=grpc with scheme=https … Rejected at validation (`400`), or invoke fails with `502 ProtocolError`.
  - Also scenario [2.4-C](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/upstreams/negative-2.4-multi-endpoint-upstream-pool-compatibility-rules.md#L53-L68) and INDEX [l.615](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L615), [l.717](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L717).
- **What the code does:**
  - [`mod.rs:138-151`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/mod.rs#L138-L151)

    ```rust
            let upstream = Upstream {
    // …
                protocol: req.protocol,
    ```

    The value is copied through untouched (update does the same at [`mod.rs:199`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/mod.rs#L199)). Nothing in `infra/proxy` or in validation reads it.
  - [`request_builder.rs:16-28`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/request_builder.rs#L16-L28)

    ```rust
            Scheme::Grpc => {
                return Err(DomainError::Validation {
                    field: "endpoint.scheme",
                    reason: field::UNSUPPORTED_SCHEME,
    ```

    So `protocol=http` with `scheme=grpc` is accepted on create and then fails at invoke time with 400 `UNSUPPORTED_SCHEME`.
  - *Likely*: `protocol=grpc` with `https` endpoints is proxied as plain HTTP.
- **Evidence of intent:** none. The `[x]` marks come from the [bulk checkbox sync](#why-the-docs-drifted). gRPC is deferred to Phase 3 ([`DESIGN.md:706`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L706)).
- **Why this decision:** The feature docs and the schema say the value must be a known protocol; validating it is cheap and stops configs that can only fail later. ADR-0002's protocol-based classification only matters once gRPC exists.
- **Impact:** Breaking for clients: create or update with any `protocol` other than the HTTP GTS id gets 400 instead of 201/200. Follow-ups: validate in [`mod.rs:143`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/mod.rs#L143) and [`mod.rs:199`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/mod.rs#L199). Document that `protocol` is not otherwise used for classification ([`DESIGN.md:704`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L704), [`ADR-0002:123-126`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0002-request-routing.md#L123-L126)). Scenario 12.7-A (400) holds; 12.7-B and [`INDEX.md:615-617`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L615-L617) “Using HTTP route against `protocol=grpc` upstream fails with `502 ProtocolError`” / [`INDEX.md:717-719`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L717-L719) “`protocol=grpc` with `scheme=https` yields `502 ProtocolError`.” become future ([STR-09](#str-09-grpc-and-webtransport-configs-are-accepted-but-grpc-traffic-never-works-and-webtransport-is-proxied-as-plain-https)).
- **Decided together with:** [STR-09](#str-09-grpc-and-webtransport-configs-are-accepted-but-grpc-traffic-never-works-and-webtransport-is-proxied-as-plain-https).

### M-07: List endpoints ignore `$filter` and other unknown query parameters and return the unfiltered list with 200

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE` + `FUTURE`, P1. Reject unsupported list query parameters (`$filter`, `$orderby`, unknown parameters) with 400 instead of returning an unfiltered 200. OData itself is planned.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`DESIGN.md:669`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L669)
    > All list endpoints support OData query parameters: `$filter`, `$select`, `$orderby`, `$top`, `$skip`.
  - [`F0002:270`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0002-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-management-api.md#L270)
    > 2. [x] - `p1` - **IF** list: Parse OData params; DB: SELECT with filters; **RETURN** 200 paginated list - `inst-route-crud-3b`
  - [`F0002:502`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0002-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-management-api.md#L502): the same doc leaves the OData acceptance criterion unticked, so it contradicts itself.
    > - [ ] OData $filter, $select, $orderby, $top (default 50, max 100), $skip supported on list endpoints; invalid syntax returns 400
  - The upstream OData parsing step [`F0002:196`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0002-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-management-api.md#L196) (`inst-list-us-3a`) is also `[ ]`.
  - [scenario 2.7](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/upstreams/positive-2.7-delete-upstream-cascades-routes.md#L22)
    > - `GET /api/oagw/v1/routes?$filter=upstream_id eq '<uuid>'` returns empty (or routes are deleted).
  - [scenario 2.9](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/upstreams/positive-2.9-tags-support-discovery-filtering.md#L32)
    > GET /api/oagw/v1/upstreams?$filter=tags has 'llm' HTTP/1.1
  - Also scenario 2.12 [step 4](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/upstreams/positive-2.12-list-upstreams-includes-disabled-resources.md#L62-L65) and scenario 3.10 [step 5](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/routes/positive-3.10-list-routes-includes-disabled-resources.md#L83-L86) (`$filter=enabled eq true`).
  - [`ADR-0010:490-492`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0010-resource-identification.md#L490-L492)
    > GET /api/oagw/v1/upstreams?tags=openai,llm … GET /api/oagw/v1/upstreams?host=api.openai.com
  - [`INDEX.md:461-489`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L461-L489) (section 25.x)
    > `GET /upstreams?$filter=alias eq 'api.openai.com'` returns only matches.
- **What the code does:**
  - [`extractors.rs:34-51`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/extractors.rs#L34-L51)

    ```rust
    pub struct PaginationQuery {
        #[serde(default = "default_top")]
        pub limit: u32,
        #[serde(default)]
        pub offset: u32,
    // …
                top: self.limit.min(100),
    ```

    Only `limit` and `offset` are parsed, plus `upstream_id` for routes ([`handlers/route.rs:61-70`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/handlers/route.rs#L61-L70)). Unknown parameters are dropped, so `$filter` returns the **unfiltered** list with 200, and a `limit` above 100 is silently clamped.
  - [`upstream_repo.rs:95`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/storage/upstream_repo.rs#L95): lists are sorted by id, a random v4 UUID (routes: [`route_repo.rs:91`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/storage/route_repo.rs#L91)). There is no `created_at`.
- **Evidence of intent:** planned. The route step was over-ticked by the [bulk checkbox sync](#why-the-docs-drifted). No test sends `$filter`.
- **Why this decision:** DESIGN describes OData, but its list section is descriptive and the feature is unbuilt, so it becomes planned. Silently ignoring a filter hands the client data it did not ask for, so the code must say "not supported" instead.
- **Impact:** Breaking for clients: requests with `$filter`, `$orderby` or other unknown parameters get 400 instead of 200 with the unfiltered list. Follow-ups: [`extractors.rs:33-52`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/extractors.rs#L33-L52), [`handlers/route.rs:61-70`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/handlers/route.rs#L61-L70). Document the `limit` clamp to 100 and the UUID ordering ([`upstream_repo.rs:95`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/storage/upstream_repo.rs#L95), no `created_at`). Future-facing markers on [`DESIGN.md:669-698`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L669-L698), scenarios 2.7, 2.9, 2.12 step 4, 3.10 step 5, [`ADR-0010:489-493`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0010-resource-identification.md#L489-L493) and [INDEX 25.x](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L461-L489). The `F0002` route OData untick is part of the status-marker sync ([O-06](#status-markers-checkbox-sync)).
- **Decided together with:** [O-06](#status-markers-checkbox-sync), [ADR-0010](#adr-0010-the-resource-identification-adr-is-still-proposed-and-describes-alias-pools-tenant-bindings-and-list-filters-that-were-never-built).

### M-08: The JSON schemas in `docs/schemas` do not match what the API accepts and returns

- **Decision:** `FIX-DOC`, no priority. Align `docs/schemas` with the wire DTOs, in the single doc-alignment PR.
- **What the docs say, and what the code does:**

  | Field | Schema (docs) | Wire (code) |
  |---|---|---|
  | `plugins.items` | [`upstream.v1:116-119`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L116-L119) “"oneOf": [ { "type": "string", "format": "gts-identifier",”; also [`route.v1:49-52`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/route.v1.schema.json#L49-L52) and [`fragment:36`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/oagw_upstream_sharing_fields.fragment.json#L36) (plain strings) | objects `{plugin_ref, config}` with a string map ([`dto.rs:253-266`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L253-L266)); plain strings fail to deserialize |
  | `auth` plugin field | flow docs use `plugin`: [`flows/plugin-execution.md:54`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/flows/plugin-execution.md#L54) “"plugin": "gts.cf.core.oagw.auth_plugin.v1~cf.core.oagw.bearer.v1",”; the schema `config` is a free-form object ([`upstream.v1:94-97`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L94-L97)) | field `type` plus `config` as a string-to-string map ([`dto.rs:63-71`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L63-L71)) |
  | `route.upstream_id` | [`route.v1:20-22`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/route.v1.schema.json#L20-L22) “"upstream_id": { "type": "string", "format": "uuid",” | GTS id `gts.cf.core.oagw.upstream.v1~<uuid>`, parsed at [`handlers/route.rs:36`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/handlers/route.rs#L36); a bare UUID gets 400 |
  | `id` | [`upstream.v1:6-8`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L6-L8) “"id": { "type": "string", "format": "uuid",” | GTS string ([`handlers/upstream.rs:17`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/handlers/upstream.rs#L17)) |
  | `endpoints[].scheme` | [`upstream.v1:40`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L40) “"enum": [ "https", "wss", "wt", "grpc" ],” (no `http`, but `wt`) | accepts `http` ([`dto.rs:32-39`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L32-L39)); the e2e suite depends on it |
  | `rate_limit` | [`upstream.v1:204-206`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L204-L206) “"rate_limit": { "type": "object", "additionalProperties": false,” with no `budget` or `response_headers`; the fragment uses flat [`rate`/`window`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/oagw_upstream_sharing_fields.fragment.json#L22-L23) | both `budget` and `response_headers` present, rate under `sustained.{rate,window}` ([`dto.rs:123-141`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L123-L141)) |
  | response `tenant_id` | absent; the upstream schema closes with [`upstream.v1:142`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L142) “"additionalProperties": false,” | present in every response ([`dto.rs:404-406`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L404-L406)) |
  | route `cors`, `priority`, `enabled` | not among the [`route.v1` properties](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/route.v1.schema.json#L5-L62) (a `cors` definition exists at l.181 but nothing references it) | present ([`dto.rs:365-382`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L365-L382)) |

- **Evidence of intent:** the OpenAPI document ([`docs/api/api.json`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/docs/api/api.json)) is generated from the DTOs and matches the code. The e2e suite and ADR-0017 use the real shape: [`ADR-0017:127`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0017-required-headers-guard-plugin.md#L127) “"plugin_ref": "gts.cf.core.oagw.guard_plugin.v1~cf.core.oagw.required_headers.v1",”. The schemas are not used at runtime.
- **Why this decision:** Schemas are a reference contract and must match the wire. Their last material change is [`7fbf9b4ea`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/7fbf9b4eab58b226473dcdf2ce28872c21c75522) (April 2026); later changes never reached them: [`216a9ccdc`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/216a9ccdc6e20b3262c46058008e30577fcba203) (GTS `upstream_id`), [`bdcf33e55`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/bdcf33e55b2d4d09ace35b613c7f4c1cb953b7cd) (plugin bindings), [`a9b28ca4a`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/a9b28ca4ad491a4f27d458d9f2c8addb04c54846) (budgets).
- **Impact:** Not breaking (docs only). Covers every row above: plugin binding shape `{plugin_ref, config}`, GTS `upstream_id`/`id`, `http` scheme, `budget`/`response_headers`, `tenant_id`, route `cors`/`priority`/`enabled`. Implemented through SCH-02…SCH-07; [SCH-01](#sch-01-types-registry-holds-empty-placeholder-schemas-for-the-oagw-types-so-it-checks-nothing-about-upstreams-routes-or-plugins) then registers the aligned schemas.
- **Decided together with:** [SCH-01](#sch-01-types-registry-holds-empty-placeholder-schemas-for-the-oagw-types-so-it-checks-nothing-about-upstreams-routes-or-plugins), [SCH-02](#sch-02-the-doc-schema-set-doesnt-match-the-registered-types-two-types-have-no-doc-schema-and-one-referenced-type-is-never-registered), [SCH-03](#sch-03-the-doc-schemas-have-no-id-so-tooling-cant-tie-them-to-a-gts-type), [SCH-04](#sch-04-the-plugin-schemas-describe-plugin-definitions-with-starlark-that-dont-exist-the-registered-plugin-instances-dont-conform), [SCH-05](#sch-05-upstreamv1schemajson-differs-from-the-upstream-shape-the-api-accepts-and-returns), [SCH-06](#sch-06-routev1schemajson-differs-from-the-route-shape-the-api-accepts-and-returns), [SCH-07](#sch-07-the-sharing-fields-fragment-has-an-old-shape).

### M-09: Documented validation rules are ticked `[x]` but missing or different in the code

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE`, P2. Implement the documented write-time validation rules; document the few rules the code has that the docs lack.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`F0002:368-376`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0002-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-management-api.md#L368-L376)
    > [x] - `p1` - **IF** match.http.path is empty - `inst-val-rt-4a` … [x] - `p1` - **IF** match.grpc.service is empty - `inst-val-rt-5a` … [x] - `p1` - **IF** priority is not a positive integer - `inst-val-rt-6`
  - [`F0001:175-186`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0001-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-domain-foundation.md#L175-L186)
    > [x] - `p1` - Validate `priority` is a non-negative integer - `inst-val-3b` … [x] - `p1` - **IF** `match_type` is `http`, validate `path_prefix` is normalized and within segment limit - `inst-val-3c` … [x] - `p1` - Validate `scheme` is `https` (HTTPS-only constraint for MVP) - `inst-val-5a` … [x] - `p1` - Validate `host` is a valid hostname or IP address - `inst-val-5b` … [x] - `p1` - Validate `port` is in valid range (1–65535) - `inst-val-5c`
  - [`ADR-0009:127`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0009-storage-schema.md#L127)
    > - For HTTP routes, `path_prefix` must be normalized and must not exceed a fixed maximum number of path segments.
  - Schema patterns: alias [`upstream.v1:19`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L19) “"pattern": "^[a-z0-9]([a-z0-9.:-]*[a-z0-9])?$",”; tags [`upstream.v1:26`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L26) “"pattern": "^[a-z0-9_-]+$"”; `methods` [`route.v1:72`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/route.v1.schema.json#L72) “"minItems": 1,”; IPv6 hosts allowed [`upstream.v1:49`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L49) “{ "format": "ipv6" }”.
- **What the code does:**
  - [`validation.rs:11-23`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/validation.rs#L11-L23): the only route rule is "exactly one of `http`/`grpc`". So the API accepts an empty or `/`-less `path`, empty `methods`, an empty gRPC service or method, a negative priority, port 0 and any tags.
  - [`alias.rs:18-37`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/alias.rs#L18-L37)

    ```rust
            .all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || matches!(c, '.' | ':' | '-' | '_'))
    // …
        if !alias.chars().any(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric()) {
    ```

    `validate_alias` allows `_` and leading or trailing punctuation.
  - [`validation.rs:40-51`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/validation.rs#L40-L51): IPv6 endpoints are *rejected* ("IPv6 endpoints are not yet supported"), a deliberate interim limit explained only in a code comment and tested ([`tests.rs:551`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/tests.rs#L551)).
  - [`validation.rs:53-59`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/validation.rs#L53-L59): pools that mix IP and hostname endpoints are rejected, a rule no doc mentions (tested at [`tests.rs:434`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/tests.rs#L434)).
- **Evidence of intent:** none for the missing rules; they were ticked by the [bulk checkbox sync](#why-the-docs-drifted). The IPv6 and mixed-pool rules are deliberate and tested.
- **Why this decision:** The feature docs, ADR-0009 and the schemas agree on these constraints; the code simply never implemented them.
- **Impact:** Breaking for clients that today send an empty or `/`-less `path`, empty `methods`, port 0, tags outside `^[a-z0-9_-]+$` or an alias with leading/trailing punctuation: they get 400 instead of 201/200. Follow-ups in [`validation.rs`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/validation.rs) and [`alias.rs:9-39`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/alias.rs#L9-L39): `path` required and starts with `/`; `methods` non-empty; port 1-65535; tags `^[a-z0-9_-]+$`; alias has no leading/trailing punctuation (`_` allowed; see [ADR-0010](#adr-0010-the-resource-identification-adr-is-still-proposed-and-describes-alias-pools-tenant-bindings-and-list-filters-that-were-never-built)). Priority: any `i32`, documented (fix [`F0001:175`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0001-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-domain-foundation.md#L175) "non-negative" and [`F0002:375`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0002-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-management-api.md#L375) "positive"). FIX-DOC: the interim IPv6 rejection ([`validation.rs:40-51`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/validation.rs#L40-L51)) and the mixed IP/hostname pool rule ([`validation.rs:53-59`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/validation.rs#L53-L59)). The gRPC service/method checks become moot under [STR-09](#str-09-grpc-and-webtransport-configs-are-accepted-but-grpc-traffic-never-works-and-webtransport-is-proxied-as-plain-https). The schema alignment ([M-08](#m-08-the-json-schemas-in-docsschemas-do-not-match-what-the-api-accepts-and-returns)) then marks these constraints as enforced.
- **Decided together with:** [M-08](#m-08-the-json-schemas-in-docsschemas-do-not-match-what-the-api-accepts-and-returns), [STR-09](#str-09-grpc-and-webtransport-configs-are-accepted-but-grpc-traffic-never-works-and-webtransport-is-proxied-as-plain-https), [ADR-0010](#adr-0010-the-resource-identification-adr-is-still-proposed-and-describes-alias-pools-tenant-bindings-and-list-filters-that-were-never-built).

### M-10: Credential references are resolved at write time for `secret_ref` only, and the docs describe a different check

- **Decision:** `FIX-DOC` + `FIX-CODE`, P2. Docs follow the code on the optional `cred://` prefix and on resolving the reference at write time (fail closed); the code validates every `*_ref` key, not only `secret_ref`.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`F0002:297-299`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0002-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-management-api.md#L297-L299)
    > 5. [x] - `p1` - **IF** auth config contains secret_ref - `inst-val-us-5` … **IF** secret_ref does not match `cred://` URI format … Add error: "Invalid secret_ref format; expected cred:// URI"
  - [`F0002:517`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0002-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-management-api.md#L517) (§7)
    > Management operations validate credential reference format but do not resolve or test credential accessibility.
  - [`DESIGN.md:138`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L138) (`principle-cred-isolation`)
    > **Credential isolation**: OAGW references secrets via `cred_store` URIs (`cred://...`); never stores or logs secret material.
  - [`F0005:378-379`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0005-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-tenant-hierarchy.md#L378-L379)
    > - [x] `secret_ref` in auth override is validated against `cred_store` for descendant tenant accessibility … - [x] Inaccessible `secret_ref` is rejected with 400 ValidationError
- **What the code does:**
  - [`bind.rs:161`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L161)

    ```rust
        let bare = raw_ref.strip_prefix("cred://").unwrap_or(raw_ref);
    ```

    The `cred://` prefix is optional.
  - [`bind.rs:165-184`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L165-L184): the reference *is* resolved through credstore on create and update. A missing secret gives 400 `failed_precondition` ([`error.rs:237-240`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/error.rs#L237-L240)); a credstore outage gives 500.
  - [`mod.rs:128-133`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/mod.rs#L128-L133)

    ```rust
            if let Some(ref auth) = req.auth
                && let Some(ref config) = auth.config
                && let Some(raw_ref) = config.get("secret_ref")
    ```

    Only the literal key `secret_ref` is checked (update: [`mod.rs:263-268`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/mod.rs#L263-L268)). The OAuth2 plugin reads `client_id_ref` and `client_secret_ref` ([`oauth2_client_cred_auth.rs:57-65`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/plugin/oauth2_client_cred_auth.rs#L57-L65)), and those are never validated.
  - The check runs with the creator's security context, but at proxy time the auth plugin resolves the secret with each caller's context ([`service.rs:1216-1220`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1216-L1220)). A secret visible only to the admin passes creation and then fails with 500 for every other caller.
- **Evidence of intent:** the accessibility check arrived with tenant hierarchy support ([`be359201c`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/be359201c532773fcecb7a55fc0344adb083696e)) and was written for the apikey plugin only. [`test_oauth2_client_cred_missing_secret_returns_error`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_oauth2_auth.py#L148-L192) creates an upstream with nonexistent OAuth2 refs successfully and expects 500 at proxy time.
- **Why this decision:** The credential-isolation security principle favours failing closed at write time, which the code already does; the feature doc text is stale. The same check must cover every reference key.
- **Impact:** Breaking for clients that create OAuth2 upstreams with refs they cannot read: they get 400 at create/update instead of 500 at proxy time. Follow-ups: FIX-DOC `F0002` inst-val-us-5 ([l.297-299](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0002-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-management-api.md#L297-L299)) and §7 [l.517](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0002-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-management-api.md#L517); document that the creator-context check is best-effort (proxy time uses the caller's context). FIX-CODE: validate OAuth2 `client_id_ref`/`client_secret_ref` as well as `secret_ref` ([`mod.rs:130`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/mod.rs#L130), [`mod.rs:265`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/mod.rs#L265), [`bind.rs:156-200`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L156-L200)). Fix [`e2e/test_oauth2_auth.py:148-192`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_oauth2_auth.py#L148-L192), which creates upstreams with nonexistent refs.

### M-11: Unknown or wrong-type plugin ids are accepted on write and only fail, or are silently skipped, at proxy time

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE`, P2. Validate plugin references at write time.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`F0003:199-204`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0003-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-plugin-system.md#L199-L204)
    > 3. [ ] - `p1` - **FOR EACH** plugin reference in bindings - `inst-bind-3` … **IF** resolution fails, **RETURN** 400 ValidationError - `inst-bind-3b` … **IF** type mismatch, **RETURN** 400 ValidationError - `inst-bind-3d` … Validate positions are contiguous from 0 with no gaps - `inst-bind-4`
  - [`ADR-0009:133-135`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0009-storage-schema.md#L133-L135)
    > - `plugin_ref` must resolve to a built-in plugin in the registry. … - `oagw_upstream_plugin` / `oagw_route_plugin` bindings must not reference auth plugins (auth is configured only via `auth_plugin_*`).
  - [`DESIGN.md:848`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L848)
    > - Plugin binding positions contiguous from 0, validated on write
  - [`DESIGN.md:314`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L314)
    > - Application validates that `plugin_uuid` matches `plugin_ref` when present.
  - [scenario 4.4](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/plugins/negative-4.4-plugin-type-enforcement.md#L31-L35) (a guard plugin id sent as `auth.type`)
    > - `400 Bad Request` … - `detail` mentions plugin type mismatch (`guard` vs `auth`).
- **What the code does:**
  - [`mod.rs:84-157`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/mod.rs#L84-L157): create checks endpoints, SSRF, CORS, budget, alias, bind rules and `secret_ref`, but stores `auth.type` and every `plugins.items[].plugin_ref` as opaque strings.
  - [`service.rs:1209-1215`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1209-L1215)

    ```rust
        let plugin = auth_registry.resolve(&auth.plugin_type).map_err(|e| {
            DomainError::AuthenticationFailed {
                reason: reason::auth::PLUGIN_NOT_FOUND,
    ```

    A guard or unknown id in `auth.type` fails only at proxy time, with 401 `PLUGIN_NOT_FOUND`.
  - [`service.rs:1502-1513`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1502-L1513): `collect_plugin_bindings` keeps only ids that match the guard or transform predicate (called at l.703 and l.718), so an auth-plugin id inside `plugins.items` is silently ignored.
  - [`model.rs:269-272`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/model.rs#L269-L272): a binding has no position field.

    ```rust
    pub struct PluginBinding {
        pub plugin_ref: String,
        pub config: HashMap<String, String>,
    }
    ```
- **Evidence of intent:** planned (`F0003` steps are `[ ]`). Two tests depend on unknown refs being accepted: [`proxy_nonexistent_auth_plugin_returns_error`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/tests/proxy_integration.rs#L928-L987) creates an upstream with a nonexistent auth plugin id and expects an authentication error at proxy time; [`test_unknown_transform_does_not_block_pipeline`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_transform_plugins.py#L128-L162) binds a nonexistent transform and expects 200.
- **Why this decision:** ADR-0009, `F0003` and scenario 4.4 all describe rejection at write time; a config that can only fail at proxy time should be rejected when it is written.
- **Impact:** Breaking for clients: create or update with an unknown, catalog-only or wrong-type plugin id gets 400 instead of succeeding. Follow-ups: 400 unless `auth.type` is an implemented auth plugin and each `plugins.items[].plugin_ref` is an implemented guard or transform plugin; catalog-only ids → 400 "not implemented" ([PLG-01](#plg-01-six-cataloged-plugins-basic-bearer-timeout-cors-logging-metrics-have-no-implementation-but-some-docs-still-promise-them)). An auth plugin inside `plugins.items` → 400 (today silently ignored, [`service.rs:1502-1513`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1502-L1513)). Update `proxy_nonexistent_auth_plugin_returns_error` ([`proxy_integration.rs:930-987`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/tests/proxy_integration.rs#L930-L987)) and [`e2e/test_transform_plugins.py:129-162`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_transform_plugins.py#L129-L162). This makes [PLG-03](#plg-03-an-unknown-plugin-id-gives-401-auth-500-guard-or-is-silently-skipped-transform-not-the-documented-503)'s 503 mostly unreachable. Docs: `F0003` inst-bind-3..4 ([`F0003:199-204`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0003-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-plugin-system.md#L199-L204)), [`ADR-0009:125-137`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0009-storage-schema.md#L125-L137), scenario 4.4. Management unit tests that create upstreams with short ids such as `"noop"` (e.g. [`tests.rs:886`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/tests.rs#L886)) will also need GTS ids.
- **Decided together with:** [PLG-03](#plg-03-an-unknown-plugin-id-gives-401-auth-500-guard-or-is-silently-skipped-transform-not-the-documented-503), [PLG-01](#plg-01-six-cataloged-plugins-basic-bearer-timeout-cors-logging-metrics-have-no-implementation-but-some-docs-still-promise-them).

### M-12: A disabled upstream silently falls through to an ancestor's upstream instead of returning 503

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE`, P1. A disabled upstream returns 503, per PRD `fr-enable-disable`. The nearest match wins; if it is disabled, return `upstream_disabled`, with no fall-through to an ancestor.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`PRD.md:217`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L217)
    > A disabled upstream **MUST** cause all proxy requests to be rejected with `503 Service Unavailable`. … If an ancestor tenant disables an upstream, it **MUST** be disabled for all descendants; descendants **MUST NOT** re-enable an ancestor-disabled resource.
  - [`F0004:173-174`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0004-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-proxy-engine.md#L173-L174)
    > 1. [x] - `p1` - **IF** upstream disabled (enabled = false) - `inst-alias-4b1` … **RETURN** error: upstream disabled (503 LinkUnavailable) - `inst-alias-4b1a`
  - [`F0004:337`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0004-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-proxy-engine.md#L337)
    > Disabled upstreams **MUST** return 503 LinkUnavailable.
  - [`upstream.v1.schema.json:15`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L15)
    > "description": "Whether this upstream is enabled. When disabled, all requests to this upstream are rejected. If a parent tenant disables an upstream, it is disabled for all descendant tenants."
  - [scenario 2.6](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/upstreams/negative-2.6-disable-upstream-blocks-proxy-traffic.md#L48-L51)
    > Descendant tenant variant … - Disable upstream at ancestor tenant. - Descendant invoking the same alias is rejected as well.
  - [`INDEX.md:581`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L581)
    > Disabled in ancestor → disabled for descendants too.
  - [`F0002:503`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0002-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-management-api.md#L503) (still unticked)
    > - [ ] Enable/disable: disabled upstream causes proxy requests to be rejected (503);
  - `F0005` agrees with the code instead: [`F0005:244`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0005-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-tenant-hierarchy.md#L244), [`F0005:325`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0005-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-tenant-hierarchy.md#L325), and [`F0005:377`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0005-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-tenant-hierarchy.md#L377)
    > - [x] Disabled upstream is skipped during resolution, falling through to next ancestor
- **What the code does:**
  - [`resolution.rs:72-77`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/resolution.rs#L72-L77)

    ```rust
                    if !upstream.enabled {
                        if disabled_alias.is_none() {
                            disabled_alias = Some(upstream.alias.clone());
                        }
                        continue;
                    }
    ```

    `resolve_alias` skips disabled upstreams and picks the closest *enabled* one; it returns `upstream_disabled` only when no enabled match exists anywhere in the chain ([`resolution.rs:85-93`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/resolution.rs#L85-L93)). A tenant that disables its own upstream is silently routed to the ancestor's endpoint.
  - [`mod.rs:391-423`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/mod.rs#L391-L423): `update_route` does not check whether the route's upstream is enabled.
- **Evidence of intent:** the fall-through is deliberate: it came with tenant hierarchy support ([`be359201c`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/be359201c532773fcecb7a55fc0344adb083696e), kept in [`c4de73b22`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/c4de73b22add1528a7c73f99165f489b9548d7dc)) and two tests encode it. [`resolve_alias_disabled_ancestor_falls_through`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/tests.rs#L868-L910) (parent disabled → resolves to root) and [`resolve_alias_disabled_child_falls_through_to_ancestor`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/tests.rs#L943-L979) (own upstream disabled → resolves to root). The e2e test [`test_disabled_upstream_returns_503_gateway`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_error_handling.py#L32-L52) is single-tenant only. The PRD and `F0004` were never updated, and no ADR covers the fall-through.
- **Why this decision:** The PRD requirement (`MUST … 503`) outranks the tests, `F0005` and the code.
- **Impact:** Breaking for clients: a tenant whose nearest upstream for an alias is disabled gets 503 instead of being served by an ancestor's upstream. Follow-ups: change [`resolution.rs:66-93`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/resolution.rs#L66-L93). Rewrite `resolve_alias_disabled_*_falls_through` ([`tests.rs:868`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/tests.rs#L868), [`tests.rs:943`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/tests.rs#L943)). FIX-DOC [`F0005:244`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0005-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-tenant-hierarchy.md#L244) “Record as disabled match; continue walking for enabled ancestor”, [`F0005:325`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0005-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-tenant-hierarchy.md#L325) “Disabled upstreams **MUST** be skipped during resolution, falling through to the next ancestor.”, [`F0005:377`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0005-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-tenant-hierarchy.md#L377). Add a multi-tenant e2e for scenario 2.6. A descendant's own shadow of an ancestor-disabled alias still wins (it is a separate resource).
- **Decided together with:** [M-03](#m-03-a-put-that-omits-tags-or-enabled-or-priority-on-routes-fails-with-422-while-docs-promise-400-and-show-such-bodies).

### M-13: Changes made through the SDK leave the load balancer and rate-limit state stale

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE`, P1. Every write path (REST and SDK) invalidates the load-balancer cache and the rate-limit keys.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [scenario 2.13](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/upstreams/positive-2.13-crud-endpoint-update-invalidates-load-balancer.md#L67-L71)
    > - CRUD operations on upstreams trigger LoadBalancer cache invalidation … - Same behavior applies to `POST` (create) and `DELETE` operations
  - [`INDEX.md:111-113`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L111-L113)
    > - **Mechanism**: `PUT /upstreams/{id}` with new endpoints invalidates cached LoadBalancer. Next proxy request lazily rebuilds with updated endpoints.
  - [`INDEX.md:89`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L89)
    > Triggers cache invalidation across CP/DP layers.
  - [`services/mod.rs:149-150`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/mod.rs#L149-L150) (code comment)
    > /// Invalidate cached state for the given upstream (called on CRUD).
- **What the code does:**
  - [`handlers/upstream.rs:98-101`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/handlers/upstream.rs#L98-L101)

    ```rust
        state.backend_selector.invalidate(upstream.id);
        if upstream.rate_limit != old_rate_limit {
            state.dp.remove_rate_limit_keys_for_upstream(uuid);
        }
    ```

    Only the REST handlers invalidate (also [`handlers/upstream.rs:43`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/handlers/upstream.rs#L43), [l.117-121](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/handlers/upstream.rs#L117-L121); [`handlers/route.rs:124-126`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/handlers/route.rs#L124-L126), [l.142](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/handlers/route.rs#L142)).
  - [`client.rs:77-80`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/client.rs#L77-L80)

    ```rust
            let internal_req = sdk_update_upstream_to_domain(req);
            self.cp
                .update_upstream(&ctx, id, internal_req)
    ```

    The SDK facade calls the control plane directly for every write ([`client.rs:17-20,71-154`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/client.rs#L17-L154)).
  - [`pingora_proxy.rs:455-458`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/pingora_proxy.rs#L455-L458)

    ```rust
            // Fast path: LB already cached.
            if let Some(entry) = self.cache.get(&upstream_id) {
                return Self::select_from_entry(&entry);
            }
    ```

    The cached load balancer is keyed by `upstream_id` and ignores the new endpoints. Result: after an SDK endpoint update, OAGW keeps routing to the old addresses until restart.
- **Evidence of intent:** invalidation was placed in the handler layer when it was introduced ([`ed93ce7bc`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/ed93ce7bcfded1a94d29e4edd35eb3c6c6790752) for the load balancer, [`a9b28ca4a`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/a9b28ca4ad491a4f27d458d9f2c8addb04c54846) for rate-limit keys). [`proxy_crud_invalidation_after_update`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/tests/proxy_integration.rs#L1526-L1614) updates through REST ([l.1589-1600](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/tests/proxy_integration.rs#L1589-L1600)); there is no SDK-path test.
- **Why this decision:** Scenario 2.13 and the code's own trait comment say CRUD invalidates; the SDK is a CRUD entry point like REST, so the behaviour belongs in the shared service.
- **Impact:** In-process SDK consumers that change upstream endpoints or rate limits. Not breaking (it removes stale routing). Follow-ups: move `backend_selector.invalidate` and `remove_rate_limit_keys_*` from [`handlers/upstream.rs`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/handlers/upstream.rs)/[`handlers/route.rs`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/handlers/route.rs) into the control-plane/domain service. Add an SDK-path test next to `proxy_crud_invalidation_after_update`.

### ADR-0010: The resource-identification ADR is still `proposed` and describes alias pools, tenant bindings and list filters that were never built

- **Decision:** `ADR` → `FIX-DOC`, no priority. Accept a revised ADR-0010 that covers what is built.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`ADR-0010:2`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0010-resource-identification.md#L2)
    > status: proposed
  - [`ADR-0010:73`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0010-resource-identification.md#L73) (Layer 2: Tenant Binding)
    > Links tenant to upstream definition. Holds tenant-specific auth config, rate limits, and plugins. Has its own UUID for log correlation.
  - [`ADR-0010:460-468`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0010-resource-identification.md#L460-L468): create returns a wrapped response.

    ```json
    {
      "upstream": {
    // …
      "binding": { "id": "binding-uuid", "created": true }
    ```

  - [`ADR-0010:326`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0010-resource-identification.md#L326) (alias pools)
    > When multiple upstreams share the same alias **within same tenant or explicitly configured for pooling**, they form a load-balance pool:
  - [`ADR-0010:103`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0010-resource-identification.md#L103)
    > | Single host | hostname (without port) | `api.openai.com:443` → `api.openai.com` |
  - [`ADR-0010:120`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0010-resource-identification.md#L120)
    > - Single endpoint: alias defaults to `server.endpoints[0].host` (without port)
  - [`ADR-0010:400-402`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0010-resource-identification.md#L400-L402): the alias pattern has no `_`.

    ```json
      "alias": {
        "type": "string",
        "pattern": "^[a-z0-9]([a-z0-9.-]*[a-z0-9])?$",
    ```

  - List filters at [`ADR-0010:489-493`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0010-resource-identification.md#L489-L493) (see [M-07](#m-07-list-endpoints-ignore-filter-and-other-unknown-query-parameters-and-return-the-unfiltered-list-with-200)); explicit aliases for hostname endpoints at [l.247-282](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0010-resource-identification.md#L247-L282) (see [M-06](#other-doc-only-fixes-management-api)).
  - Parts that already match the code: [`ADR-0010:206-209`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0010-resource-identification.md#L206-L209)
    > The alias is immutable once set — regardless of whether it was auto-derived (hostname-based) or explicitly provided (IP-based).
- **What the code does:**
  - [`model.rs:75-82`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/model.rs#L75-L82)

    ```rust
        pub fn alias_contribution(&self) -> String {
            let host = self.normalized_host();
            if self.is_standard_port() {
                host
            } else {
                format!("{host}:{}", self.port)
    ```

    A non-standard port derives `host:port`, not the bare hostname. So the ADR's cross-port shadowing under one alias cannot happen.
  - [`alias.rs:158-167`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/alias.rs#L158-L167): an explicit alias on hostname endpoints is rejected with 400 (only IP-based endpoints take an explicit alias); [`mod.rs:218-231`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/mod.rs#L218-L231): the alias is immutable for every upstream type.
  - [`upstream_repo.rs:38-46`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/storage/upstream_repo.rs#L38-L46): at most one upstream per `(tenant, alias)`; a second one gets a conflict, so alias pools cannot exist.
  - There is no binding entity: a bind is simply a descendant upstream with the same alias ([`bind.rs:202-225`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L202-L225)). Responses are flat objects ([`dto.rs:403-423`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L403-L423)).
  - [`alias.rs:18-25`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/alias.rs#L18-L25): `validate_alias` allows `_`, which the ADR pattern forbids (see [M-09](#m-09-documented-validation-rules-are-ticked-x-but-missing-or-different-in-the-code)).
- **Evidence of intent:** alias enforcement ([`15cbef9bc`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/15cbef9bc5bd52eb978d935bc14e78b04b3eb0f0)) and alias immutability ([`2f6c9ee77`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/2f6c9ee77831e5571d273f524c6d7bcfd1381c6e)) were deliberate and are tested (see [M-05](#other-doc-only-fixes-management-api), [M-06](#other-doc-only-fixes-management-api)).
- **Why this decision:** A `proposed` ADR that disagrees with the code is stale. Accept a revised version that records what was built, and move the unbuilt parts to "future".
- **Impact:** Not breaking (docs only). Follow-ups: keep derived `host:port` aliases, immutability, and explicit aliases only for IP endpoints. Move to "future": alias pools ([l.324-345](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0010-resource-identification.md#L324-L345)), the Tenant Binding entity and the wrapped `{upstream, binding}` response ([l.71-97](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0010-resource-identification.md#L71-L97), [l.416-469](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0010-resource-identification.md#L416-L469)), and the list filters ([l.489-493](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0010-resource-identification.md#L489-L493)). Fix the alias pattern ([l.402](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0010-resource-identification.md#L402), which forbids the `_` the code allows; see [M-09](#m-09-documented-validation-rules-are-ticked-x-but-missing-or-different-in-the-code)) and the port-derivation wording ([l.103](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0010-resource-identification.md#L103)/[l.120](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0010-resource-identification.md#L120)). Status → `accepted` (the ADR status fixes are tracked under [A-04](#adr-statuses)). The ADR-0010 parts of [M-06](#other-doc-only-fixes-management-api) and [M-17](#other-doc-only-fixes-management-api) are done through this revision.
- **Decided together with:** [M-05](#other-doc-only-fixes-management-api), [M-06](#other-doc-only-fixes-management-api), [M-07](#m-07-list-endpoints-ignore-filter-and-other-unknown-query-parameters-and-return-the-unfiltered-list-with-200), [M-09](#m-09-documented-validation-rules-are-ticked-x-but-missing-or-different-in-the-code), [M-17](#other-doc-only-fixes-management-api), [A-04](#adr-statuses).

## 4. Proxy engine (data plane)

How OAGW matches, rewrites, forwards and fails requests to `/api/oagw/v1/proxy/{alias}/…`.

Mechanical doc fixes in this area: [P-13](#other-doc-only-fixes-proxy), [P-14](#other-doc-only-fixes-proxy), [P-15](#other-doc-only-fixes-proxy), [P-17](#other-doc-only-fixes-proxy), and the doc part of [P-07](#p-07-error-type-ids-field-names-and-resource-identifiers-dont-match-what-oagw-sends) (see [Mechanical doc fixes](#mechanical-doc-fixes)).

### P-01: A route prefix matches mid-segment, so `/v1/chatty` reaches the upstream as `/v1/chat/ty`

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE`, P1. A route path matches only at a segment boundary: the request path equals the prefix or continues with `/`.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`DESIGN.md:527`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L527)
    > | Path suffix | Path | Append to `match.http.path` if `path_suffix_mode`: `append`; plugin mutable |
  - [`ADR-0009:389-390`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0009-storage-schema.md#L389-L390)
    > This query assumes the application precomputes a bounded list of candidate prefixes for the request path (longest first), e.g. `/a/b/c` -> [`/a/b/c`, `/a/b`, `/a`, `/`].
  - [`F0004:192`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0004-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-proxy-engine.md#L192)
    > [x] - `p1` - DB: Check request path starts with path_prefix from oagw_route_http_match - `inst-route-3a2`
