  - Scenarios and INDEX bind or describe these ids: [scenario 9.3](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/proxy-api/authentication/positive-9.3-basic-auth-injection.md#L15) “cf.core.oagw.basic.v1”, [scenario 9.4](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/proxy-api/authentication/positive-9.4-bearer-token-passthrough-injection.md#L15) “cf.core.oagw.bearer.v1”, [scenario 10.1](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/plugins/guards/negative-10.1-timeout-guard-plugin-enforces-request-timeout.md#L5-L6) “Attach builtin timeout guard plugin to the route”, [scenario 4.5-A](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/plugins/positive-4.5-plugin-resolution-supports-builtin-named-ids-custom-uuid.md#L6) “cf.core.oagw.logging.v1”, [scenario 4.6-A](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/plugins/positive-4.6-plugin-sharing-modes-merge-correctly-across-tenant-hierarchy.md#L25) “cf.core.oagw.metrics.v1”, [example-01](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/protocols/http/examples/positive-example-01-http-request-response.md#L52) “cf.core.oagw.logging.v1”, [`INDEX.md:179-185`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L179-L185) “Correct `Authorization: Basic ...` formatting from secret.”
  - Already correct: [`ADR-0003:130`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0003-plugin-system.md#L130)
    > Their GTS identifiers (`cf.core.oagw.timeout.v1`, `cf.core.oagw.cors.v1`) exist for types-registry cataloging only and are not resolvable via `GuardPluginRegistry`.
  - Already correct: [`DESIGN.md:396`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L396)
    > **Catalog-only identifiers** (reserved GTS ids, not resolvable via a plugin registry — `basic`/`bearer` have no backing implementation; `timeout`/`cors`/`logging`/`metrics` are core Data Plane logic instead):
  - Already correct: [`PRD.md:284-295`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L284-L295) “HTTP Basic authentication; catalog identifier only, no backing `AuthPlugin` implementation”
- **What the code does:**
  - [`registry.rs:34-65`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/plugin/registry.rs#L34-L65): registers only the apikey, noop, oauth2_client_cred and oauth2_client_cred_basic auth plugins.
  - [`registry.rs:94-98`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/plugin/registry.rs#L94-L98) and [`registry.rs:133-137`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/plugin/registry.rs#L133-L137): the only guard is `required_headers`, the only transform is `request_id`.
  - [`type_catalog.rs:46-69`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/type_catalog.rs#L46-L69): all 12 plugin GTS ids are registered in the types-registry.
- **Evidence of intent:** [`e8158be6b`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/e8158be6bbdef9bbcc5605b25bfb4f5181ddd0fc) fixed the plugin lists in ADR-0003, DESIGN and PRD, but not in F0003, DECOMPOSITION, the flow doc, INDEX or the scenarios ([partial doc syncs](#why-the-docs-drifted)). The "11" count predates the `required_headers` guard. [`test_oagw_types_registered.py`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_oagw_types_registered.py) checks the catalogue entries, not that the plugins run.
- **Why this decision:** The accepted ADR-0003 already says these ids are catalog-only. Unbuilt features get a planned marker; they are not built.
- **Impact:** Not breaking; docs only. Finish the doc sync: [`F0003:348-350`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0003-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-plugin-system.md#L348-L350), [`F0003:425`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0003-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-plugin-system.md#L425), [`DECOMPOSITION:183`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DECOMPOSITION.md#L183), [`plugin-execution.md:54-81`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/flows/plugin-execution.md#L54-L81), [`plugin-execution.md:419-431`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/flows/plugin-execution.md#L419-L431), [`PRD:171`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L171), [`PRD:237`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L237), [`INDEX:179-185`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L179-L185). Scenarios [9.3](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/proxy-api/authentication/positive-9.3-basic-auth-injection.md), [9.4](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/proxy-api/authentication/positive-9.4-bearer-token-passthrough-injection.md), [10.1](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/plugins/guards/negative-10.1-timeout-guard-plugin-enforces-request-timeout.md), [4.5-A](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/plugins/positive-4.5-plugin-resolution-supports-builtin-named-ids-custom-uuid.md)/[4.6](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/plugins/positive-4.6-plugin-sharing-modes-merge-correctly-across-tenant-hierarchy.md) and [example-01](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/protocols/http/examples/positive-example-01-http-request-response.md) get future-facing markers. The [`PRD:237`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L237) MUST (Basic and Bearer supported) gets a planned marker, which needs product sign-off. Whether binding a catalog-only id is rejected at write time is decided under [M-11](#m-11-unknown-or-wrong-type-plugin-ids-are-accepted-on-write-and-only-fail-or-are-silently-skipped-at-proxy-time).
- **Decided together with:** [M-11](#m-11-unknown-or-wrong-type-plugin-ids-are-accepted-on-write-and-only-fail-or-are-silently-skipped-at-proxy-time), [PLG-03](#plg-03-an-unknown-plugin-id-gives-401-auth-500-guard-or-is-silently-skipped-transform-not-the-documented-503).

### PLG-02: Route plugins with the default `sharing: private` replace the upstream's plugins instead of running after them

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE`, P1. Route plugins always run after the upstream's plugins, per ADR-0003 and DESIGN, whatever the route's `sharing`.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`ADR-0003:112`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0003-plugin-system.md#L112)
    > Upstream plugins execute before route plugins.
  - [`DESIGN.md:389`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L389)
    > Plugin chain composition: upstream plugins execute before route plugins (`[U1, U2] + [R1, R2] => [U1, U2, R1, R2]`).
  - [`F0004:361-363`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0004-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-proxy-engine.md#L361-L363)
    > - [x] `p1` - **ID**: `cpt-cf-oagw-dod-plugin-chain` … Upstream plugins **MUST** execute before route plugins (`[U1, U2] + [R1, R2]`).
  - [scenario 11.6](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/plugins/transforms/positive-11.6-plugin-ordering-layering.md#L30) “Observable order string equals `U1,U2,R1,R2`.”
  - [`plugin-execution.md:390`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/flows/plugin-execution.md#L390)
    > **Merge strategy**: Union (all plugins execute), upstream first.
  - [scenario 4.6-A](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/plugins/positive-4.6-plugin-sharing-modes-merge-correctly-across-tenant-hierarchy.md#L19-L32) (the child's plugins have no `sharing`, so they default to `private`)
    > 2. Child tenant binds to the upstream and adds plugins: … Expected effective chain order: - `[parent.logging, child.metrics]`
  - The one doc that describes "replace", for tenant layers: [`F0005:221`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0005-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-tenant-hierarchy.md#L221)
    > **IF** `plugins_sharing == private` and no ancestor enforce, replace with descendant plugins (local-only)
- **What the code does:**
  - [`merge.rs:109-118`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L109-L118): the comment says "concatenate", the code below it replaces.

    ```rust
            // Route plugins: concatenate upstream + route plugins.
    // …
                SharingMode::Private => {
                    // Private → route's plugins replace upstream's (local-only).
                    effective.plugins = Some(crate::domain::model::PluginsConfig {
    ```
  - [`dto.rs:260-266`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L260-L266): `plugins.sharing` defaults to `private` when the client omits it ([`dto.rs:17-24`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L17-L24)).
  - [`merge.rs:119-139`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L119-L139): for `inherit`/`enforce`, items are de-duplicated by `plugin_ref` and the route's config overwrites, so the same plugin cannot run twice with different configs.
  - [`merge.rs:436-437`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L436-L437): the tenant-layer merge replaces the same way for a `private` descendant.
- **Evidence of intent:** [`route_private_plugins_replace_upstream`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/tests.rs#L2334-L2378) asserts the effective list is only `["route-plugin"]`. No e2e test. No doc describes the replace rule for routes; the default-private semantics came after the scenarios were written.
- **Why this decision:** The accepted ADR-0003 and DESIGN both say upstream plugins run first and route plugins are added; they outrank F0005 and the code comment.
- **Impact:** Breaking for clients: a route that sets plugins without `sharing` (or with `private`) will also run the upstream's plugins, so configs that rely on replace change behaviour. Storage is in-memory, so this affects provisioning configs, not stored data. Follow-ups: change [`merge.rs:111-118`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L111-L118); rewrite `route_private_plugins_replace_upstream` ([`tests.rs:2335`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/tests.rs#L2335)); FIX-DOC [`F0005:221`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0005-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-tenant-hierarchy.md#L221) (inst-merge-3a3); add e2e for scenarios [11.6](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/plugins/transforms/positive-11.6-plugin-ordering-layering.md) and [4.6-A](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/plugins/positive-4.6-plugin-sharing-modes-merge-correctly-across-tenant-hierarchy.md#L19-L32). The enforce-bypass part is fixed under [R-05](#r-05-descendants-and-routes-can-bypass-an-ancestors-enforced-plugins-and-rate-limit) path 3. Open: de-duplication by `plugin_ref` blocks running the same plugin twice with different configs; decide in the fix PR.
- **Decided together with:** [R-05](#r-05-descendants-and-routes-can-bypass-an-ancestors-enforced-plugins-and-rate-limit) (path 3).

### PLG-03: An unknown plugin id gives 401 (auth), 500 (guard) or is silently skipped (transform), not the documented 503

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE`, P2. An unknown plugin id of any kind (auth, guard, transform) fails closed with 503 `plugin_not_found`.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`DESIGN.md:730`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L730)
    > PluginNotFound | 503 | `gts.cf.core.errors.err.v1~cf.oagw.plugin.not_found.v1` | No | Plugin not found
  - [`F0004:219-220`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0004-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-proxy-engine.md#L219-L220), ticked by the [bulk checkbox sync](#why-the-docs-drifted)
    > [x] - `p1` - **IF** plugin not found - `inst-chain-4d` … [x] - `p1` - **RETURN** 503 PluginNotFound with `X-OAGW-Error-Source: gateway` - `inst-chain-4d1`
  - [`INDEX.md:157`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L157)
    > UUID plugin id requires DB row; missing yields `503 PLUGIN_NOT_FOUND`.
  - [scenario 4.5-B](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/plugins/positive-4.5-plugin-resolution-supports-builtin-named-ids-custom-uuid.md#L19-L23)
    > Expected: - `503 Service Unavailable` … - Error type `gts.cf.core.errors.err.v1~cf.oagw.plugin.not_found.v1`
- **What the code does:**
  - [`service.rs:1209-1215`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1209-L1215): an unknown auth plugin becomes an authentication failure, so the client gets 401.

    ```rust
        let plugin = auth_registry.resolve(&auth.plugin_type).map_err(|e| {
            DomainError::AuthenticationFailed {
                reason: reason::auth::PLUGIN_NOT_FOUND,
    ```
  - [`service.rs:1272-1280`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1272-L1280) (and the response phase, [`service.rs:1534-1542`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1534-L1542)): an unknown guard becomes an internal error, so every request on that route gets 500. Binding `timeout.v1` or `cors.v1` breaks the route.
  - [`service.rs:1361-1368`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1361-L1368): an unknown transform is logged as a warning and skipped; the request succeeds, so `logging.v1`/`metrics.v1` do nothing.
  - [`error.rs:351-361`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/error.rs#L351-L361): a `PluginNotFound` error type already exists, but it maps to a 404 `not_found` per plugin kind (asserted by [`plugin_not_found_dispatches_on_gts_prefix`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/error.rs#L678-L686)), and the proxy pipeline never raises it.
- **Evidence of intent:** Each executor chose its own semantics when guards and transforms were added in [`bdcf33e55`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/bdcf33e55b2d4d09ace35b613c7f4c1cb953b7cd). Tests lock in two of them: [`test_unknown_transform_does_not_block_pipeline`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_transform_plugins.py#L128-L160) asserts 200, and [`proxy_nonexistent_auth_plugin_returns_error`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/tests/proxy_integration.rs#L928-L989) asserts an `Unauthenticated` error. No test covers an unknown guard.
- **Why this decision:** DESIGN's error table, F0004 and the scenario agree on 503, and failing closed is the safe default when a configured plugin is missing.
- **Impact:** Breaking for clients: requests with an unknown auth plugin get 503 instead of 401, unknown guards 503 instead of 500, and a route with an unknown transform now fails instead of succeeding. Update [`test_transform_plugins.py:129`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_transform_plugins.py#L129) (asserts 200). [M-11](#m-11-unknown-or-wrong-type-plugin-ids-are-accepted-on-write-and-only-fail-or-are-silently-skipped-at-proxy-time) (write-time validation) makes this path mostly unreachable.
- **Decided together with:** [M-11](#m-11-unknown-or-wrong-type-plugin-ids-are-accepted-on-write-and-only-fail-or-are-silently-skipped-at-proxy-time), [PLG-01](#plg-01-six-cataloged-plugins-basic-bearer-timeout-cors-logging-metrics-have-no-implementation-but-some-docs-still-promise-them).

### PLG-05: Guards check the gateway's outbound headers (after passthrough filtering and auth), not the headers the client sent

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE`, P1. Request-phase guards see the inbound request headers, as the `GuardContext` doc and ADR-0017 say.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`plugin/mod.rs:87-89`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/plugin/mod.rs#L87-L89) (the `GuardContext` rustdoc)
    > During the request phase, `status` is `None` and `headers` contains the … inbound request headers.
  - [`ADR-0017:64-68`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0017-required-headers-guard-plugin.md#L64-L68) (status: accepted)
    > A stateless `RequiredHeadersGuardPlugin` checks for the presence of configured header names on the request (before proxying to upstream)
- **What the code does:**
  - [`service.rs:705-714`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L705-L714): guards get the outbound header map.

    ```rust
            execute_guard_requests(
                &self.guard_registry,
                &guard_bindings,
                &outbound_headers,
    ```
  - [`headers.rs:27-28`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/headers.rs#L27-L28): under the default `passthrough: none` that map starts empty (only `Content-Type` is copied).

    ```rust
        let mut out = match mode {
            PassthroughMode::None => HeaderMap::new(),
    ```
  - [`headers.rs:13-18`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/headers.rs#L13-L18): `authorization`, `cookie`, `proxy-authorization` and `set-cookie` are always removed from it.
  - [`service.rs:679-693`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L679-L693): the auth plugin then writes its credential into the same map, before the guards run.
  - Result: with the default headers config, `required_request_headers: x-correlation-id` rejects every request, even when the client sends the header. A guard that requires `authorization` is always satisfied by the injected credential.
- **Evidence of intent:** The outbound map was reused when guards were added in [`bdcf33e55`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/bdcf33e55b2d4d09ace35b613c7f4c1cb953b7cd); nothing documents it. Every "allow" test sets `passthrough: all`, which hides the problem: [`test_guard_plugins.py:49`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_guard_plugins.py#L49), [`:91`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_guard_plugins.py#L91), [`:164`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_guard_plugins.py#L164), and in Rust [`proxy_integration.rs:2955`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/tests/proxy_integration.rs#L2955).
- **Why this decision:** The accepted ADR-0017 and the `GuardContext` contract both say the guard checks the request. A guard that the gateway's own credential can satisfy does not check the caller.
- **Impact:** Operators with `required_headers` guards see requests that were wrongly rejected under default passthrough start passing. Breaking for clients: a guard that requires `authorization` now needs the client to send it. Fix: pass `req_headers` instead of `outbound_headers` ([`service.rs:705-714`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L705-L714)). Add tests with the default `passthrough=none` (current tests all set `passthrough: all`).
- **Decided together with:** [P-11](#p-11-with-the-default-passthrough-the-upstream-never-sees-the-clients-x-request-id-accept-or-trace-headers), [O-04](#o-04-no-audit-log-config-change-log-or-log-sampling-and-the-trace-breaks-at-oagw) (P-11 notes the request_id plugin has the same root cause).

### PLG-10: A missing secret gives 500, while an unreachable credential store or IdP gives 401, and the status differs by plugin

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE`, P2. One credential-failure mapping for all auth plugins.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`F0003:436`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0003-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-plugin-system.md#L436)
    > If resolution fails, OAGW returns `401 AuthenticationFailed`.
  - [`PRD.md:629`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L629)
    > - **Auth plugin fails**: Return 401 AuthenticationFailed
  - [scenario 9.7-A](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/proxy-api/authentication/negative-9.7-secret-access-control-cred-store.md#L20-L23)
    > Invoke proxy as a tenant without secret access. Expected: - `401 Unauthorized`
  - [scenario 9.7-B](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/proxy-api/authentication/negative-9.7-secret-access-control-cred-store.md#L29-L32)
    > Use `secret_ref` that does not exist. Expected: - `500 Internal Server Error` with `type` = `...secret.not_found...`.
- **What the code does:**
  - [`oauth2_client_cred_auth.rs:162-167`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/plugin/oauth2_client_cred_auth.rs#L162-L167) and [`apikey_auth.rs:49-54`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/plugin/apikey_auth.rs#L49-L54): a cred_store error becomes `Internal`; "no secret" becomes `SecretNotFound`.

    ```rust
            .map_err(|e| PluginError::Internal(format!("credstore error: {e}")))?
            .ok_or_else(|| PluginError::SecretNotFound(config.secret_ref.clone()))?;
    ```
  - [`error.rs:224-230`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/error.rs#L224-L230): `SecretNotFound` → 500 `internal`. cred_store answers "no secret" both when access is denied and when the secret is missing, so the client cannot tell them apart.
  - [`service.rs:1247-1251`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1247-L1251): `PluginError::Internal` → 401. That covers cred_store errors, IdP failures ([`oauth2_client_cred_auth.rs:213-215`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/plugin/oauth2_client_cred_auth.rs#L213-L215)), malformed refs and a bad apikey config ([`apikey_auth.rs:36-47`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/plugin/apikey_auth.rs#L36-L47)). An IdP outage looks like a caller auth failure.
  - [`service.rs:1231-1239`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1231-L1239): a bad oauth2 config (`InvalidConfig`) → 400, while a bad apikey config → 401.
- **Evidence of intent:** [`test_oauth2_auth.py:189-192`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_oauth2_auth.py#L189-L192) asserts 500 for missing secrets. [`test_auth_injection.py:33-56`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_auth_injection.py#L33-L56) skips on 400/401/500 instead of failing.
- **Why this decision:** The PRD and F0003 both say a credential resolution failure is 401, and an outage of a dependency must not look like a caller error.
- **Impact:** The new mapping: resolution failure (missing or denied) → 401 `AuthenticationFailed` ([`PRD:629`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L629), [`F0003:436`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0003-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-plugin-system.md#L436)); credstore/IdP unavailable → 503 gateway, retryable; bad IdP response → 502; plugin misconfiguration → 500 (and 400 at write time via [M-10](#m-10-credential-references-are-resolved-at-write-time-for-secret_ref-only-and-the-docs-describe-a-different-check)/[M-11](#m-11-unknown-or-wrong-type-plugin-ids-are-accepted-on-write-and-only-fail-or-are-silently-skipped-at-proxy-time)). Breaking for clients: a missing secret changes 500 → 401, a cred_store or IdP outage 401 → 503, a bad apikey config 401 → 500, a bad oauth2 config 400 → 500. Touches [`error.rs:224-230`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/error.rs#L224-L230), [`service.rs:1247-1251`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1247-L1251), [`apikey_auth.rs:36-47`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/plugin/apikey_auth.rs#L36-L47), [`oauth2_client_cred_auth.rs:162-170`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/plugin/oauth2_client_cred_auth.rs#L162-L170), [`oauth2_client_cred_auth.rs:213-215`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/plugin/oauth2_client_cred_auth.rs#L213-L215). Rewrite [scenario 9.7-B](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/proxy-api/authentication/negative-9.7-secret-access-control-cred-store.md#L27-L32); fix [`test_oauth2_auth.py:189-192`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_oauth2_auth.py#L189-L192); remove the soft-skips in [`test_auth_injection.py:33-56`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_auth_injection.py#L33-L56).
- **Decided together with:** [M-10](#m-10-credential-references-are-resolved-at-write-time-for-secret_ref-only-and-the-docs-describe-a-different-check), [M-11](#m-11-unknown-or-wrong-type-plugin-ids-are-accepted-on-write-and-only-fail-or-are-silently-skipped-at-proxy-time).

### PLG-12: CORS without `allowed_methods` rejects every cross-origin request, while the docs default it to GET and POST

- **Decision:** `FUTURE` (was `FIX-CODE` P2), no priority. Superseded by [PLG-13](#plg-13-route-level-cors-is-ignored-unless-sharing-inherit-and-a-descendants-own-private-or-enforce-cors-is-dropped) (CORS is deferred). When CORS is re-implemented, `allowed_methods` defaults to `["GET","POST"]`, per [`ADR-0006:176-180`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0006-cors.md#L176-L180).
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`ADR-0006:176-180`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0006-cors.md#L176-L180) (status: accepted)
    > "allowed_methods": { … "default": [ "GET", "POST" ],
  - [`upstream.v1.schema.json:294-297`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L294-L297)
    > "allowed_methods": { … "default": [ "GET", "POST" ],
- **What the code does:**
  - [`dto.rs:241-242`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L241-L242): an omitted `allowed_methods` becomes an empty list.

    ```rust
        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
        pub allowed_methods: Vec<CorsHttpMethod>,
    ```
  - [`cors.rs:137-140`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/cors.rs#L137-L140): a method is allowed only if it is in that list, and [`service.rs:1397-1402`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1397-L1402) returns 403 otherwise. So `{"enabled":true,"allowed_origins":[…]}` rejects every actual cross-origin request with 403.
- **Evidence of intent:** none. Every test sets `allowed_methods` explicitly ([`test_cors.py:8-14`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_cors.py#L8-L14)).
- **Why this decision:** The accepted ADR-0006 and the schema define the default. The fix is kept for when CORS returns, because [PLG-13](#plg-13-route-level-cors-is-ignored-unless-sharing-inherit-and-a-descendants-own-private-or-enforce-cors-is-dropped) removes CORS now.
- **Impact:** Nothing changes now. When CORS returns: the default at [`dto.rs:241-242`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L241-L242), and the [`upstream.v1.schema.json:294-297`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L294-L297) `allowed_methods.default` (keep `["GET","POST"]` in the [M-08](#m-08-the-json-schemas-in-docsschemas-do-not-match-what-the-api-accepts-and-returns) schema alignment).
- **Decided together with:** [PLG-13](#plg-13-route-level-cors-is-ignored-unless-sharing-inherit-and-a-descendants-own-private-or-enforce-cors-is-dropped), [PLG-14](#other-doc-only-fixes-plugins), [PLG-15](#other-doc-only-fixes-plugins).

### PLG-13: Route-level CORS is ignored unless `sharing: inherit`, and a descendant's own private or enforce CORS is dropped

- **Decision:** `FUTURE` + `FIX-CODE`, P2. Take CORS out of OAGW and defer it: `cors` on an upstream or route is rejected at write time and the CORS code is removed, because the current implementation has several issues ([PLG-12](#plg-12-cors-without-allowed_methods-rejects-every-cross-origin-request-while-the-docs-default-it-to-get-and-post), PLG-13, [PLG-14](#other-doc-only-fixes-plugins), [PLG-15](#other-doc-only-fixes-plugins)).
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`ADR-0006:1-7`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0006-cors.md#L1-L7) “status: accepted … CORS — Built-in Handler with Per-Upstream/Route Configuration”
  - [`ADR-0006:74`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0006-cors.md#L74)
    > CORS is a first-class field in upstream/route configuration:
  - [scenario 10.2](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/plugins/guards/positive-10.2-built-cors-handling.md#L5)
    > Enable CORS on upstream or route:
  - [`DESIGN.md:282`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L282)
    > CORS is configured via the dedicated `cors` field on `Upstream`/`Route`
  - [`F0005:223`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0005-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-tenant-hierarchy.md#L223), the step the code follows
    > [x] - `p2` - Merge CORS: **IF** `cors_sharing == private`, skip; **IF** field is absent, inherit from previous level; **IF** `cors_sharing: inherit`, union origins; **IF** `cors_sharing: enforce`, use ancestor CORS - `inst-merge-3a5`
  - [`route.v1.schema.json:5-62`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/route.v1.schema.json#L5-L62): the route's top-level properties have no `cors` (a `definitions.cors` block at l.181 is never referenced), although the route DTO accepts it ([`dto.rs:375`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L375)).
- **What the code does:**
  - [`merge.rs:164-167`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L164-L167): route CORS with `private` (the default) or `enforce` is skipped.

    ```rust
                    match route_cors.sharing {
                        SharingMode::Private | SharingMode::Enforce => {
                            // Private → skip; Enforce → use upstream CORS.
                        }
    ```
  - [`merge.rs:327-333`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L327-L333): a descendant upstream's own `private` or `enforce` CORS is skipped; the ancestor's CORS (or none) applies.
  - [`handlers/proxy.rs:33-72`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/handlers/proxy.rs#L33-L72): any `OPTIONS` with `Origin` and `Access-Control-Request-Method` gets a 204 before upstream resolution. Routes cannot match `OPTIONS` or `HEAD` today: [`model.rs:287-293`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/model.rs#L287-L293) lists only `Get`, `Post`, `Put`, `Delete`, `Patch`.
  - Browser CORS is also available at the platform level, in api-gateway: [`api-gateway/src/config.rs:43-46`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/api-gateway/src/config.rs#L43-L46)

    ```rust
        pub cors_enabled: bool,
        /// Optional detailed CORS configuration
        #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
        pub cors: Option<CorsConfig>,
    ```
- **Evidence of intent:** CORS and F0005 inst-merge-3a5 were added together in [`70b81ab49`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/70b81ab49c827d394bb369d08b8f8edb3590e8b2); the permissive preflight came in [`7fbf9b4ea`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/7fbf9b4eab58b226473dcdf2ce28872c21c75522). Tests lock in today's behaviour: [`effective_config_cors_private_descendant_skips`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/tests.rs#L1388-L1408) asserts a private child's origins are dropped; for routes only `inherit` is covered ([`cors_route_inherit_merges_origins`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/tests/proxy_integration.rs#L3820-L3822)); [`test_cors.py`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_cors.py) covers preflight and actual requests end to end.
- **Why this decision:** The merge code follows F0005 inst-merge-3a5 literally, and that step contradicts the accepted ADR-0006 ("per-upstream/route"). Instead of fixing merging, defaults and preflight one by one (PLG-12…PLG-15), CORS is deferred; browser CORS stays with the api-gateway platform layer, and ADR-0006 stays the target design.
- **Impact:** Breaking for clients: an upstream or route create/update that sends `cors` gets 400 "not supported yet"; OAGW no longer answers preflights or adds CORS headers; `OPTIONS` is proxied like any other method. Browser clients that rely on OAGW CORS today must use the api-gateway CORS layer. This goes against the accepted ADR-0006, which gets a "deferred" status note. It changes behaviour locked in by [`test_cors.py`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_cors.py) and the Rust CORS tests (such as `effective_config_cors_private_descendant_skips`). Order: the HEAD/OPTIONS routing change in [P-18](#p-18-the-query-string-is-re-encoded-headoptions-cant-be-routed-and-the-host-port-rule-ignores-the-scheme) must land before the preflight handler is removed, or OPTIONS requests have no route to match.
  - FIX-CODE: `cors` on an upstream or route → 400 "not supported yet". Remove [`src/domain/cors.rs`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/cors.rs), the CORS merge ([`merge.rs:155-182`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L155-L182) and [`merge.rs:293-351`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L293-L351)), `enforce_cors_origin` ([`service.rs:579-592`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L579-L592)) and the preflight short-circuit ([`handlers/proxy.rs:33-72`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/handlers/proxy.rs#L33-L72)). Remove or xfail [`test_cors.py`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_cors.py) and the Rust CORS tests.
  - FIX-DOC: ADR-0006 stays the target design with a "deferred" status note; mark `cors` not supported in the [`upstream.v1`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L137-L140)/[`route.v1`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/route.v1.schema.json#L181-L190) schemas; scenarios 10.x ([`plugins/guards/`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/tree/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/plugins/guards)) get a future-facing marker; [`DESIGN:282`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L282), [`F0005:223`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0005-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-tenant-hierarchy.md#L223) (inst-merge-3a5), [`F0008:228`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0008-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-observability.md#L228) “**RETURN** 403 with CORS origin not allowed error (request not forwarded to upstream)”, [`F0008:364`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0008-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-observability.md#L364) “Configuration **MUST** support: `allowed_origins`, `allowed_methods`.”, [`INDEX:365-373`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L365-L373) “OPTIONS preflight returns permissive `204` at handler level (no upstream resolution).”
  - [PLG-12](#plg-12-cors-without-allowed_methods-rejects-every-cross-origin-request-while-the-docs-default-it-to-get-and-post) is superseded; [PLG-14](#other-doc-only-fixes-plugins) and [PLG-15](#other-doc-only-fixes-plugins) go with it.
- **Decided together with:** [PLG-12](#plg-12-cors-without-allowed_methods-rejects-every-cross-origin-request-while-the-docs-default-it-to-get-and-post), [PLG-14](#other-doc-only-fixes-plugins), [PLG-15](#other-doc-only-fixes-plugins), [P-18](#p-18-the-query-string-is-re-encoded-headoptions-cant-be-routed-and-the-host-port-rule-ignores-the-scheme).

### PLG-17: Headers written by auth and transform plugins are not re-checked for hop-by-hop names (*Likely*)

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE`, P2. Strip hop-by-hop headers again after auth and transforms, just before the request is sent.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`INDEX.md:397`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L397)
    > Hop-by-hop headers remain stripped even if plugin tries to set them.
  - [scenario 11.3](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/plugins/transforms/positive-11.3-header-mutation-transform.md#L28-L30)
    > - Hop-by-hop headers remain stripped even if plugin tries to set them (lock expected behavior).
- **What the code does:**
  - [`service.rs:644-649`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L644-L649): stripping happens once, in step 4 of the pipeline.

    ```rust
            if is_upgrade {
                headers::strip_hop_by_hop_for_upgrade(&mut outbound_headers);
            } else {
                headers::strip_hop_by_hop(&mut outbound_headers);
            }
    ```
  - [`service.rs:693`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L693) (auth output) and [`service.rs:1373`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1373) (transform output) replace the header map later; nothing strips again. No built-in plugin sets a hop-by-hop header today.
- **Evidence of intent:** none.
- **Why this decision:** INDEX and scenario 11.3 state the invariant; re-stripping before send enforces it for any future plugin.
- **Impact:** Not breaking; defensive, since no built-in plugin triggers it today. Strip again after the writes at [`service.rs:693`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L693) and [`service.rs:1373`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1373) (today's strip is at [`service.rs:644-649`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L644-L649)). [Scenario 11.3:28-30](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/plugins/transforms/positive-11.3-header-mutation-transform.md#L28-L30), [`INDEX:397`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L397).

## 6. Rate limiting, budgets, tenant hierarchy and sharing

This area covers how rate limits, budgets and the `private`/`inherit`/`enforce` sharing modes behave across a tenant hierarchy, and how the limiter keys and resets its buckets.

Mechanical doc fixes in this area: [R-18](#status-markers-checkbox-sync) (see [Mechanical doc fixes](#mechanical-doc-fixes)).

### R-01: A child cannot set any rate limit under an `enforce` ancestor, not even a stricter one

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE`, P1. Accept a child `rate_limit` under an ancestor with `rate_limit.sharing=enforce`; the effective limit is `min(parent, child)`, as the PRD and ADR-0004 say.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`ADR-0004:322`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0004-rate-limiting.md#L322)
    > | `enforce`      | any             | `min(parent, child)` - cannot exceed parent |
  - [`PRD.md:336`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L336)
    > - **Rate limits**: Descendant can only be stricter: `effective = min(ancestor.enforced, descendant)`
  - [`PRD.md:349-353`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L349-L353) (hierarchical override example)
    > rate_limit: { rate: 10000/min, sharing: "enforce" } … rate_limit: { rate: 100/min }  <- effective: min(10000, 100) = 100
  - [`PRD.md:428-431`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L428-L431)
    > Effective for sub: min(root.enforced:10000, sub:500) = 500
  - [`DESIGN.md:562`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L562)
    > | `oagw:upstream:override_rate` | Specify own rate limits (subject to min()) |
  - [`F0006:140-141`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0006-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-rate-limiting.md#L140-L141)
    > 4. [x] - `p2` - **IF** ancestor has `sharing: enforce` on rate limit - `inst-cfg-4` … 1. [x] - `p2` - Validate descendant's limit does not exceed ancestor's enforced limit - `inst-cfg-4a`
  - [scenario 18.6](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/rate-limiting/positive-18.6-hierarchical-min-merge-descendant-overrides.md#L5-L29): parent `enforce` 1000/min, child 100/min.
    > Effective limit for child is `min(parent, child)`.
  - [scenario negative-6.2](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/proxy-api/alias-resolution/negative-6.2-enforced-ancestor-limits-still-apply-alias-shadowed.md#L5-L18)
    > Effective rate limit for child is `min(parent_enforced, child)`.
- **What the code does:**
  - [`bind.rs:98-104`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L98-L104): any child `rate_limit` under an `enforce` ancestor gets 400, whatever its value.

    ```rust
    if overrides.rate_limit.is_some() {
        match ancestor.rate_limit.as_ref().map(|r| r.sharing) {
            Some(SharingMode::Enforce) => {
                return Err(DomainError::validation(
                    "cannot override rate_limit: ancestor upstream has sharing mode 'enforce'",
    ```
  - The check runs on both create ([`mod.rs:112`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/mod.rs#L112)) and update ([`mod.rs:247`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/mod.rs#L247)).
  - The `min()` branch for a child under `enforce` already exists ([`merge.rs:256-259`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L256-L259)). Today it is reachable only if the ancestor switches to `enforce` after the child exists.
- **Evidence of intent:** none for the rejection. It dates from [`be359201c`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/be359201c532773fcecb7a55fc0344adb083696e) (tenant hierarchy support). The `[x]` on `inst-cfg-4a` was set in [`a9b28ca4a`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/a9b28ca4ad491a4f27d458d9f2c8addb04c54846) without reconciling it. No e2e test covers scenario 18.6 or negative-6.2. The Rust test `update_removing_rate_limit_still_validates_against_ancestor` hedges at [`tests.rs:2092-2096`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/tests.rs#L2092-L2096): “Create may fail due to enforce — that's fine, the point is the update path.” The merge unit tests (for example [`effective_config_rate_limit_min_wins`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/tests.rs#L1116-L1129)) build chains the API refuses to create.
- **Why this decision:** the PRD and the accepted ADR-0004 both say a child under `enforce` gets `min(parent, child)`. Nothing records a decision to block the child instead.
- **Impact:** tenant admins can set a stricter limit under an enforced parent; today they get 400. Not breaking (a request that failed now succeeds). Follow-ups: [`bind.rs:99-104`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L99-L104) stops rejecting; add e2e tests for scenarios 18.6 and negative-6.2; remove the hedge at [`tests.rs:2092-2096`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/tests.rs#L2092-L2096).
- **Decided together with:** [R-02](#r-02-a-child-cannot-be-created-under-an-enforce-parent-that-has-an-allocated-budget).

### R-02: A child cannot be created under an `enforce` parent that has an allocated budget

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE` (via R-01) + `FIX-DOC`, P1. The R-01 fix resolves this. Keep the rule "a child `rate_limit` is required under `budget.mode=allocated`" and document it.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [scenario 18.7-A](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/rate-limiting/positive-18.7-budget-modes-behave-specified.md#L3-L20): the parent has `enforce` and an allocated budget; children are accepted up to the total.
    > "sharing": "enforce", … "budget": { "mode": "allocated", "total": 100, "overcommit_ratio": 1.0 }, … Creation/update rejected when `sum(children) > total * overcommit_ratio`.
  - [`ADR-0004:527-569`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0004-rate-limiting.md#L527-L569) (Example 1): system and partner both use `enforce` with an allocated budget, and a tenant sets its own limit.
    > "sharing": "enforce", … "budget": { "mode": "allocated", "total": 5000, "overcommit_ratio": 1.2 } … **Tenant level** (allocated 1000/min by partner):
- **What the code does:**
  - A child that sends `rate_limit` is rejected by the `enforce` check ([R-01](#r-01-a-child-cannot-set-any-rate-limit-under-an-enforce-ancestor-not-even-a-stricter-one)).
  - A child that omits it is rejected by [`budget.rs:80-86`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/budget.rs#L80-L86). No doc describes this rule.

    ```rust
    if child_rate_limit.is_none() {
        return Err(DomainError::validation(
            "rate_limit is required when ancestor has budget.mode = 'allocated'",
    ```
- **Evidence of intent:** the required-`rate_limit` rule is deliberate and tested: e2e [`test_allocated_rejects_child_without_rate_limit`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_budget_allocation.py#L406-L431) asserts 400 and “rate_limit is required”. All e2e budget tests use `inherit` parents (for example [`test_budget_allocation.py:161`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_budget_allocation.py#L161)), so none hits the `enforce` combination.
- **Why this decision:** the accepted ADR-0004 Example 1 uses exactly this combination. Once R-01 lets a child send its own limit, the allocation rule works as intended.
- **Impact:** operators can build the ADR-0004 System → Partner → Tenant setup. Not breaking. Follow-ups: add an e2e test for scenario 18.7-A with an `enforce` parent; document the [`budget.rs:82-86`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/budget.rs#L82-L86) rule in ADR-0004 and `F0006`.
- **Decided together with:** [R-01](#r-01-a-child-cannot-set-any-rate-limit-under-an-enforce-ancestor-not-even-a-stricter-one).

### R-03: Under an `inherit` ancestor, a child can raise its own limit above the ancestor's

- **Decision:** `ADR` → `FIX-DOC`, no priority. Keep the code's model: `inherit` is a default that a child with the `override_rate` permission may override (and may raise); `enforce` is a ceiling applied with `min()`.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`ADR-0004:315-322`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0004-rate-limiting.md#L315-L322) (inheritance rules)
    > | `inherit`      | own limit       | `min(parent, child)`                        |
  - [`DESIGN.md:410`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L410)
    > | Rate Limits | `min(ancestor, descendant)` — stricter always wins |
  - [`PRD.md:336`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L336) caps a descendant only against an **enforced** ancestor, which matches the code:
    > `effective = min(ancestor.enforced, descendant)`
  - [`F0005:220`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0005-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-tenant-hierarchy.md#L220) (`inst-merge-3a2`) also matches the code:
    > **IF** `rate_limit_sharing == private` and no ancestor enforce, replace with descendant rate (local-only)
- **What the code does:**
  - The sharing mode defaults to `private` ([`model.rs:13`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/model.rs#L13), [`dto.rs:124-125`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L124-L125)).
  - A `private` child with no enforcing ancestor replaces the limit ([`merge.rs:261-263`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L261-L263)). Parent `inherit` 100/min plus child 1000/min gives 1000/min.

    ```rust
    SharingMode::Private => {
        effective.rate_limit = Some(descendant_rl.clone());
    }
    ```
  - `min()` applies only when the child explicitly sets `inherit` or `enforce` ([`merge.rs:264-266`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L264-L266)).
  - Setting a limit under an `inherit` ancestor needs the `override_rate` permission ([`bind.rs:106-116`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L106-L116)).
- **Evidence of intent:** the bind check asks for `override_rate` under `inherit`, which only makes sense if the child may set a different limit. `F0005` `inst-merge-3a2` describes the same model. No test asserts the raise itself.
- **Why this decision:** the PRD already caps only against enforced ancestors, and the code and `F0005` agree with it. The accepted ADR-0004 row and [`DESIGN.md:410`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L410) are the outliers, so they are amended rather than the code changed.
- **Impact:** no client-visible change. Not breaking. Follow-ups: amend ADR-0004 (inheritance table [l.315-325](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0004-rate-limiting.md#L315-L325), the `inherit` + own-limit row); FIX-DOC [`DESIGN.md:410`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L410) ("stricter always wins"). The case "a `private` parent still caps an `inherit` child" is handled under [R-04](#r-04-an-ancestors-private-auth-rate-limit-and-plugins-reach-descendants-that-omit-the-field).
- **Decided together with:** [R-04](#r-04-an-ancestors-private-auth-rate-limit-and-plugins-reach-descendants-that-omit-the-field).

### R-04: An ancestor's `private` auth, rate limit and plugins reach descendants that omit the field

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE`, P0. An ancestor's `private` auth, rate limit and plugins never propagate to descendants, even when the descendant omits the field.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`PRD.md:330`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L330)
    > | `private` | Not visible to descendants (default) |
  - [`INDEX:233`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L233)
    > Descendant cannot see ancestor `private` configuration.
  - [scenario 9.8-C](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/proxy-api/authentication/positive-9.8-hierarchical-auth-sharing-modes-behave-specified.md#L39-L44)
    > Parent sets `auth.sharing=private`. … Child must provide its own auth.
  - [scenario 4.6-C](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/plugins/positive-4.6-plugin-sharing-modes-merge-correctly-across-tenant-hierarchy.md#L45-L51)
    > `plugins.sharing=private` … Child does not inherit parent plugins.
  - [`F0005:221`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0005-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-tenant-hierarchy.md#L221) is ambiguous and contradicts scenario 4.6-C:
    > Merge plugins: **IF** field is absent, inherit ancestor plugins;
- **What the code does:**
  - An ancestor joins the merge chain if any one of its fields is not `private` ([`merge.rs:14-35`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L14-L35), used at [`resolution.rs:69`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/resolution.rs#L69)).
  - The merge starts from a whole copy of the root upstream, `private` fields included ([`merge.rs:61`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L61)).

    ```rust
    let mut effective = ancestor_chain[0].clone();
    ```
  - An absent descendant field means "keep the previous value" for auth ([`merge.rs:215`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L215)), rate limit ([`merge.rs:254`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L254)) and plugins ([`merge.rs:416`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L416)).
  - Example: parent `auth` `private` + `rate_limit` `inherit`, child with no auth. The child's effective upstream carries the parent's auth plugin and `secret_ref`, which the proxy then injects ([`service.rs:677`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L677)).
  - A `private` parent rate limit also caps an `inherit` child: the `inherit` arm takes `min()` with whatever is in effect, whatever its sharing mode ([`merge.rs:264-266`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L264-L266)).
  - Only CORS drops a `private` ancestor value when the descendant omits the field ([`merge.rs:314-323`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L314-L323), added in [`70b81ab49`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/70b81ab49c827d394bb369d08b8f8edb3590e8b2)).

    ```rust
    None => {
        // Absent → inherit from previous level, but Private must not propagate.
    ```
  - Mitigation for auth: the secret is fetched with the descendant's security context, so `cred_store` may still refuse it (*Likely*).
- **Evidence of intent:** only the CORS case is tested ([`effective_config_cors_private_ancestor_not_inherited_when_absent`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/tests.rs#L1464-L1476) asserts “Private ancestor CORS must not propagate to descendants”). Nothing tests or documents the leak for the other fields.
- **Why this decision:** security first. The PRD defines `private` as "not visible to descendants"; a parent's credentials must not flow to a child that never asked for them.
- **Impact:** descendants that relied on a `private` ancestor field reaching them lose it (for example, a child with no auth now sends no credentials). Breaking for operators who relied on the leak. Follow-ups: generalise the CORS strip-private-on-absence rule ([`merge.rs:314-323`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L314-L323)) to `merge_auth`, `merge_rate_limit` and `merge_plugins` ([`merge.rs:215`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L215), [`merge.rs:254`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L254), [`merge.rs:416`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L416)); stop cloning the root whole ([`merge.rs:61`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L61)); same PR as R-05. This includes the "`private` parent caps an `inherit` child" case from [R-03](#r-03-under-an-inherit-ancestor-a-child-can-raise-its-own-limit-above-the-ancestors).
- **Decided together with:** [R-05](#r-05-descendants-and-routes-can-bypass-an-ancestors-enforced-plugins-and-rate-limit) (same PR), [R-03](#r-03-under-an-inherit-ancestor-a-child-can-raise-its-own-limit-above-the-ancestors).

### R-05: Descendants and routes can bypass an ancestor's enforced plugins and rate limit

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE`, P0. Close all four enforce-bypass paths.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`PRD.md:337`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L337)
    > - **Plugins**: Descendant's plugins append; enforced plugins cannot be removed
  - [`F0005:199`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0005-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-tenant-hierarchy.md#L199)
    > 1. [x] - `p2` - Ensure enforced plugins remain in chain; descendant cannot remove them - `inst-enforce-3c1`
  - [`F0005:226`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0005-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-tenant-hierarchy.md#L226) (layering note for `inst-merge-2`)
    > Step 3 applies tenant hierarchy overrides last (tenant > route), ensuring tenant `enforce` constraints are never bypassed.
  - [scenario 4.6-B](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/management-api/plugins/positive-4.6-plugin-sharing-modes-merge-correctly-across-tenant-hierarchy.md#L34-L43)
    > Child cannot remove/replace parent plugins.
- **What the code does:** four paths.
  1. An `inherit` or `enforce` descendant overwrites an enforced plugin's config when the `plugin_ref` matches, and resets `sharing` to its own mode ([`merge.rs:439-459`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L439-L459)).

     ```rust
     if let Some(existing) =
         merged.iter_mut().find(|m| m.plugin_ref == item.plugin_ref)
     {
         *existing = item.clone();
     …
         sharing: descendant_plugins.sharing,
     ```

     So root `enforce` → child `inherit` → grandchild `private` replaces the whole chain, because the `private` arm replaces whatever is no longer marked enforced ([`merge.rs:436-437`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L436-L437)).
  2. Bind validation deliberately skips plugins under `enforce` and only asks for the `add_plugins` permission ([`bind.rs:120-136`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L120-L136)). It checks only the closest ancestor with the alias ([`bind.rs:214-219`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L214-L219)).
  3. A route with `private` plugins (the default) replaces even enforced upstream plugins; there is no enforce check ([`merge.rs:111-118`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L111-L118)).

     ```rust
     SharingMode::Private => {
         // Private → route's plugins replace upstream's (local-only).
     ```

     The route merge runs after the tenant chain, and `create_route` does no hierarchy checks ([`mod.rs:330-368`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/mod.rs#L330-L368)). Even `items: []` removes an enforced guard. Route CORS does check enforce ([`merge.rs:159-163`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L159-L163)).
  4. `merge_rate_limit` has the same shape ([`merge.rs:264-266`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L264-L266)). It is reachable if the root switches to `enforce` after a child has bound with its own limit, because an update does not revalidate descendants' own limits; it re-checks descendants only for budget totals ([`mod.rs:278-292`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/mod.rs#L278-L292)).
- **Evidence of intent:** defence in depth exists only for the `private` arm. Tests cover `private` under `enforce` ([`merge_plugins_private_cannot_drop_enforced_ancestor_plugins`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/tests.rs#L2452), [`merge_plugins_enforced_ancestor_config_wins_on_ref_conflict`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/tests.rs#L2482)) and `enforce` + `enforce` ([`effective_config_enforced_plugins_cannot_be_removed`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/tests.rs#L1194)). There is no three-level test and no test of a `private` route over enforced upstream plugins.
- **Why this decision:** security first, and the PRD says enforced plugins cannot be removed. An enforced guard that a grandchild or a route can drop gives no protection.
- **Impact:** descendants and routes can no longer drop or reconfigure enforced plugins; some bind requests that pass today will be rejected. Breaking for configs that rely on a bypass. Follow-ups: (1) a descendant can't overwrite or reset an enforced plugin ([`merge.rs:443-459`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L443-L459)); (2) bind validates the full ancestor chain and enforced plugins ([`bind.rs:120-136`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L120-L136), [`bind.rs:214-219`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L214-L219)), which also settles the closest-ancestor-only check from [R-16](#r-16-f0005-bind-and-permission-rules-differ-from-the-code); (3) the route merge honours enforce ([`merge.rs:111-118`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L111-L118)), which is also how [PLG-02](#plg-02-route-plugins-with-the-default-sharing-private-replace-the-upstreams-plugins-instead-of-running-after-them)'s enforce-bypass part is fixed; (4) a rate limit under an ancestor switched to `enforce` is handled at merge time. Add three-level tests and route-`private`-over-`enforce` tests. Same PR as R-04.
- **Decided together with:** [R-04](#r-04-an-ancestors-private-auth-rate-limit-and-plugins-reach-descendants-that-omit-the-field) (same PR), [PLG-02](#plg-02-route-plugins-with-the-default-sharing-private-replace-the-upstreams-plugins-instead-of-running-after-them) (path 3), [R-16](#r-16-f0005-bind-and-permission-rules-differ-from-the-code) (closest-ancestor check).

### R-06: Requests with a different merged limit reset the shared bucket, so callers bypass limits

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE`, P1. Stop per-request bucket resets on the same key.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`F0006:358`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0006-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-rate-limiting.md#L358)
    > Route-level rate limits **MUST** apply as `min(upstream_effective, route_config)`.
  - [`ADR-0004:313`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0004-rate-limiting.md#L313)
    > | `shared`    | Children share parent's budget (first-come-first-served) |
- **What the code does:**
  - When the stored bucket's parameters differ from the request's config, the limiter replaces it with a fresh, full bucket ([`rate_limit.rs:393-405`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/rate_limit.rs#L393-L405)).

    ```rust
    .and_modify(|bucket| {
        if !bucket.matches_config(config) {
            *bucket = match config.algorithm {
                RateLimitAlgorithm::TokenBucket => Bucket::Token(TokenBucket::new(config)),
    ```
  - The upstream bucket key uses the upstream id (or the pool owner id), but the config under that key varies per request. A route with `inherit`/`enforce` is min-merged into the upstream's effective limit ([`merge.rs:145-153`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L145-L153)), which is then checked against the upstream key ([`service.rs:1446-1463`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1446-L1463)). The route limit is also checked in its own bucket ([`service.rs:1465-1476`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1465-L1476)).
  - Example: upstream 100/min, route A with no limit, route B inheriting 50/min. Every request to B resets the upstream bucket, so A is effectively unbounded.
  - Shared pools whose children have their own `inherit` limits reset each other the same way ([`merge.rs:284-290`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L284-L290), [`merge.rs:369-371`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L369-L371)).
- **Evidence of intent:** the reset arrived with the limiter in [`a9b28ca4a`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/a9b28ca4ad491a4f27d458d9f2c8addb04c54846) to pick up admin edits, not per-request variation. No test covers it; the route tests use the default `private` sharing.
- **Why this decision:** `F0006` and the accepted ADR-0004 require upstream limits and shared pools to hold across routes and tenants. A limit that any caller can reset is not a limit.
- **Impact:** clients that got through because of resets will now get 429. Follow-ups: check the upstream bucket only against the upstream's own effective config; the route-merged min stays in the route's own bucket ([`merge.rs:145-153`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L145-L153), [`service.rs:1465-1476`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1465-L1476)). On a genuine config change, clamp the tokens instead of refilling ([`rate_limit.rs:397-404`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/rate_limit.rs#L397-L404)). Test: upstream 100/min + route A (no limit) + route B (`inherit` 50/min).
