Follow-ups: mark ADR-0015 `superseded` (also listed under [A-04](#adr-statuses)); new ADR covering `ServiceGatewayClientV1`, `http::Request/Response<oagw_sdk::Body>`, `ServerEventsStream`, `WebSocketStream`, `ServiceGatewayError`/`StreamingError`, `Json`/`MultipartBody` and the `axum` feature; a FUTURE note for a remote (OoP) client if still wanted; fix [`DESIGN:946`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L946), [`DESIGN:89`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L89) “`ServiceGatewayClientV1` trait, SDK models, error types” and [`DESIGN:332`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L332) “Public API: ServiceGatewayClientV1 trait, models, errors” (the §10 "SDK shape" row).

### STR-13: The SDK SSE parser does not follow the W3C rules it claims to follow

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE`, P2. Make the SDK SSE parser W3C-compliant.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`event.rs:3`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw-sdk/src/sse/event.rs#L3)
    > Follows the W3C EventSource specification fields.
  - [`event.rs:8`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw-sdk/src/sse/event.rs#L8)
    > The `event` field. Defaults to "message" if omitted by the server.
- **What the code does:**
  - [`parse.rs:147-157`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw-sdk/src/sse/parse.rs#L147-L157): at EOF, an incomplete trailing block (no closing blank line) is still dispatched as an event. The W3C spec says to discard it.

    ```rust
    if state.done {
        // Stream is finished. Flush any remaining data in the buffer.
        if !state.buf.trim().is_empty() {
    // …
            if !event.is_empty() {
                return Some((Ok(event), state));
    ```
  - [`event.rs:17-21`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw-sdk/src/sse/event.rs#L17-L21): a block counts as an event if it has any field, so blocks with only `id:` or `retry:` are yielded as events; W3C dispatches only when there is data.
  - [`parse.rs:52-53`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw-sdk/src/sse/parse.rs#L52-L53): `event` is set only from an `event:` line, so it stays `None` instead of defaulting to `"message"`.
- **Evidence of intent:** the unit tests assert today's behaviour: [`flush_trailing_event_without_final_newlines`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw-sdk/src/sse/parse.rs#L331-L344) ("Some servers don't send the trailing \n\n"), [`metadata_only_block_yields_event`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw-sdk/src/sse/parse.rs#L616-L633) ("SDK yields it (consumers decide)") and [`parse_single_event`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw-sdk/src/sse/parse.rs#L238-L252) (`event` is `None`).
- **Why this decision:** The SDK's own documentation promises W3C behaviour; the parser follows the spec it names rather than the reverse.
- **Impact:** SDK consumers of `ServerEventsStream`: a truncated final event is no longer delivered, `id`/`retry`-only blocks no longer appear as events, and `event` reads `"message"` when the server omits it. Code that matches `event == None` must change. Follow-ups: [`sse/parse.rs:147-157`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw-sdk/src/sse/parse.rs#L147-L157) (discard an incomplete trailing block at EOF), [`sse/event.rs:17-21`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw-sdk/src/sse/event.rs#L17-L21) (don't yield id/retry-only blocks as events; `event` defaults to `"message"`); rewrite the three tests above.

### STR-14: Streaming tests pass without checking the behaviour they are named after

- **Decision:** `FIX-DOC` (tests), no priority. Strengthen the streaming tests.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`F0007:309`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0007-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-streaming.md#L309) (acceptance criterion, ticked but untested)
    > [x] SSE fields (`data:`, `event:`, `id:`, `retry:`) are preserved as-is during forwarding
  - [`F0007:320`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0007-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-streaming.md#L320)
    > [x] Idle timeout on WebSocket session sends 1001 Going Away to both sides
- **What the code does:**
  - [`proxy_integration.rs:4449-4452`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/tests/proxy_integration.rs#L4449-L4452) and [`proxy_integration.rs:2132-2136`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/tests/proxy_integration.rs#L2132-L2136): both WS idle tests accept EOF as a pass, so they pass even if no Close 1001 is sent.

    ```rust
    if let Some((op, _)) = read_ws_frame(&mut stream).await {
        // Close frame received; EOF (None) is also valid — gateway closed TCP.
        assert_eq!(op, 0x8, "expected close frame after idle timeout");
    }
    ```
  - [`test_sse_streaming.py:27-37`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/oagw/test_sse_streaming.py#L27-L37): the SSE e2e test reads `resp.text` fully buffered, so it cannot detect buffering. No test checks that `event:`, `id:` or `retry:` are preserved.
  - There is no WS-over-TLS (`wss`) coverage, no SSE mid-stream abort test and no full-stack SSE idle test.
  - [`field.rs:35-44`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw-sdk/src/field.rs#L35-L44) and the error field name `x-target-host` differ from the real header `x-oagw-target-host` ([`service.rs:278`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L278)); that fix is decided under [P-07](#p-07-error-type-ids-field-names-and-resource-identifiers-dont-match-what-oagw-sends).
- **Evidence of intent:** none.
- **Why this decision:** Tests are evidence, not authority; these ones assert less than the acceptance criteria they claim to cover.
- **Impact:** Test-only; not breaking. Follow-ups: WS idle tests must require Close 1001 ([`proxy_integration.rs:2107-2139`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/tests/proxy_integration.rs#L2107-L2139), [`proxy_integration.rs:4413-4456`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/tests/proxy_integration.rs#L4413-L4456)); the SSE e2e test must read the stream incrementally; add `event:`/`id:`/`retry:` preservation ([AC l.309](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0007-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-streaming.md#L309)), WS-over-TLS, SSE mid-stream abort and full-stack SSE idle tests. The `x-target-host` field name is under P-07 (code).
- **Decided together with:** related to [STR-02](#str-02-sse-and-websocket-streams-are-cut-after-30-s-of-upstream-silence-not-after-the-300-s-idle-timeouts-likely), [STR-05](#str-05-a-websocket-upgrade-to-an-https-upstream-fails-whenever-tls-negotiation-picks-http2-likely), [STR-01](#str-01-a-mid-stream-upstream-failure-appends-problem-json-to-a-200-sse-stream-likely).

## 8. Observability

OAGW emits a basic set of OpenTelemetry metrics and one Pingora completion log line; the docs describe a full Prometheus, audit-log and tracing design.

Mechanical doc fixes in this area: [O-02](#other-doc-only-fixes-streaming-sdk-and-observability), [O-06](#status-markers-checkbox-sync) (see [Mechanical doc fixes](#mechanical-doc-fixes)).

### O-01: Operators get 6 of the 12 documented metrics, pushed over OTLP; there is no `/metrics` endpoint

- **Decision:** `WONTFIX` (endpoint) + `FIX-DOC` + `FUTURE`, no priority. Metrics stay OTLP push with no `/metrics` endpoint, the docs are corrected to say so, and the 6 missing metrics are marked planned with their features.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`F0008:321-324`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0008-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-observability.md#L321-L324)
    > The system **MUST** expose all 12 OAGW Prometheus metrics at the admin `/metrics` endpoint:
  - [`F0008:129-136`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0008-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-observability.md#L129-L136) (the "Review Prometheus Metrics" flow)
    > Unauthorized access to metrics endpoint (401) … Operator sends GET /metrics to the admin endpoint
  - [`PRD.md:524`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L524)
    > **Threshold**: 100% of proxy requests logged with correlation ID; metrics scraped at /metrics endpoint
  - [`DESIGN.md:882`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L882)
    > Prometheus metrics at `/metrics` (admin-only):
  - [`DECOMPOSITION.md:507`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DECOMPOSITION.md#L507)
    > None (integrated into proxy flow and admin `/metrics` endpoint)
  - [`INDEX.md:427-429`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L427-L429)
    > Section 22.1 — no separate file. Verified as part of management auth scenarios. … Unauthenticated `GET /metrics` rejected. Non-admin rejected.
- **What the code does:**
  - [`metrics.rs:51-60`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/metrics.rs#L51-L60): six of the documented instruments exist, plus two undocumented WebSocket ones (`active_websocket_sessions`, `websocket_session_duration_seconds`).

    ```rust
        requests: Counter<u64>,
        errors: Counter<u64>,
        request_duration_seconds: Histogram<f64>,
        requests_in_flight: UpDownCounter<i64>,
        rate_limit_exceeded: Counter<u64>,
        rate_limit_usage_ratio: Gauge<f64>,
    ```
  - [`metrics.rs:3-6`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/metrics.rs#L3-L6): says the circuit-breaker, routing and upstream-availability metrics are deferred until their features land.
  - [`init.rs:391-427`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/libs/toolkit/src/telemetry/init.rs#L391-L427): the platform telemetry layer builds an OTLP (HTTP or gRPC) metric exporter with a periodic push. OAGW registers no `/metrics` route.
  - [`config.rs:70-92`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L70-L92): the metric name prefix is configurable (`metrics.prefix`, default derived from the gear name). No doc mentions it.
- **Evidence of intent:** commit [`204b7ed7e`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/204b7ed7e521722697034998455958ac90be93c9) ("feat(oagw): emit basic proxy metrics") added exactly this set and the "deferred" note. [`configurable_prefix_changes_all_instrument_names`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/metrics_tests.rs#L249-L260) asserts that a custom prefix renames every instrument.
- **Why this decision:** the PRD asks for a scrape endpoint, but the platform exports metrics only by OTLP push, so OAGW follows the platform. The PRD threshold at l.524 changes as part of the doc fix, which needs product sign-off.
- **Impact:** operators see no behaviour change. Not breaking. Follow-ups:
  - FIX-DOC [`F0008:319-334`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0008-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-observability.md#L319-L334), [`F0008:430`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0008-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-observability.md#L430) and the flow at [l.119-140](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0008-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-observability.md#L119-L140) (drop the 401 case), [`DESIGN:880-905`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L880-L905), [`DECOMPOSITION:472`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DECOMPOSITION.md#L472) “`oagw_circuit_breaker_transitions_total`, `oagw_rate_limit_usage_ratio`, `oagw_routing_target_host_used`” and [`DECOMPOSITION:507`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DECOMPOSITION.md#L507), [`PRD:524`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L524), [`INDEX:427-429`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L427-L429).
  - Document the configurable prefix and the 2 WebSocket metrics.
  - FUTURE: circuit-breaker, routing and upstream-availability metrics.

### O-03: Gateway-rejected requests are missing from `requests_total`, and the in-flight and rate-limit gauges are skewed

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE`, P2. Make the metrics follow the documented pipeline: count every request with its status, count in-flight from request start, and report rate-limit usage per bucket.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`F0008:156`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0008-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-observability.md#L156)
    > Increment `oagw_requests_in_flight{host}` gauge at request start
  - [`F0008:162`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0008-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-observability.md#L162)
    > Increment `oagw_requests_total{host, http.request.method, http.route, http.response.status_code}` counter
  - [`F0008:326`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0008-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-observability.md#L326)
    > status-class queries (e.g. `5xx` rate) are expressed at query time over the numeric code.
  - [`F0008:165`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0008-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-observability.md#L165)
    > Update `oagw_rate_limit_usage_ratio{host, path}` gauge with current token ratio (0.0–1.0)
- **What the code does:**
  - [`service.rs:881-884`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L881-L884): only a successful `Ok(resp)` records a status. A gateway error (401, 429, 502, …) records only the error variant.

    ```rust
            match &result {
                Ok(resp) => guard.set_success(resp.status().as_u16()),
                Err(err) => guard.set_error(domain_error_type_name(err)),
            }
    ```
  - [`service.rs:506-513`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L506-L513): on drop, success goes to `record_request`; errors go to `record_error(host, path, variant)`, which has no status label. So a 5xx-rate query over `requests_total` misses every gateway 5xx.
  - [`metrics.rs:25-30`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/ports/metrics.rs#L25-L30): the port comment states this behaviour.

    ```rust
        /// Incremented once per completed proxy request (success or upstream
        /// 4xx/5xx). Pre-resolution failures (e.g. policy denial before
        /// upstream lookup) are NOT counted here; they go to
        /// [`Self::record_error`] only.
    ```
  - [`service.rs:479-484`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L479-L484): the in-flight gauge is incremented in `mark_resolved`, which runs only after upstream resolution ([l.577](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L577)).
  - [`service.rs:500-505`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L500-L505): the duration is recorded when `proxy_request` returns the response, so it measures time to response headers, not to the end of a streamed body.
  - [`service.rs:1432-1444`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1432-L1444): the upstream bucket ([l.1460](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1460)) and the route bucket ([l.1476](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1476)) both write `rate_limit_usage_ratio` under the same `host`/`path` labels, so the last write wins.
  - [`test_support.rs:649`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/test_support.rs#L649) and [`service.rs:2016`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L2016): the proxy test harnesses inject `NoopMetrics`, so no test checks what the pipeline records. [`metrics_tests.rs`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/metrics_tests.rs) tests the meter on its own.
- **Evidence of intent:** the port comment above describes today's behaviour, but no test asserts it at the pipeline level.
- **Why this decision:** the feature doc (`F0008`) defines the pipeline and outranks a code comment. [U-4](#u-4-on_error-transforms-never-run-for-upstream-error-responses-and-metrics-count-those-responses-as-successes) leaves its error-metrics part to this fix.
- **Impact:** operators notice. `requests_total` grows to include gateway 4xx/5xx, in-flight counts rise slightly, and rate-limit usage gains a per-bucket split; dashboards and alerts built on today's numbers shift. Not breaking for clients. Follow-ups: document the duration as time-to-headers; consider a non-noop metrics test harness ([`test_support.rs:649`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/test_support.rs#L649)).
- **Decided together with:** [U-4](#u-4-on_error-transforms-never-run-for-upstream-error-responses-and-metrics-count-those-responses-as-successes).

### O-04: No audit log, config-change log or log sampling, and the trace breaks at OAGW

- **Decision:** `FUTURE` + `FIX-CODE`, P2. Audit log, config-change log and sampling are marked planned; trace propagation and a richer completion log are fixed now.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`PRD.md:522`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L522)
    > The system **MUST** log all proxy requests with correlation IDs and expose Prometheus metrics for request counts, latencies, error rates, and rate limit state.
  - [`PRD.md:727`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L727)
    > - [ ] Complete audit trail for all proxy requests (correlation ID, timestamps, status)
  - [`F0008:340-348`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0008-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-observability.md#L340-L348)
    > The system **MUST** emit structured JSON logs to stdout for all proxy requests. … The `trace_id` field **MUST** be propagated from inbound W3C Trace Context (`traceparent` header) when present … The system **MUST** support high-frequency sampling to prevent excessive log volume on high-traffic routes.
  - [`DESIGN.md:918`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L918)
    > **Config changes**: Upstream/route create/update/delete operations
  - [`INDEX.md:447-449`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L447-L449) (sections 23.1-23.3)
    > Proxy requests produce structured audit log … Log includes request_id, tenant_id, principal_id, host, path, status, duration.
- **What the code does:**
  - [`pingora_proxy.rs:903-921`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/pingora_proxy.rs#L903-L921): a basic completion log already exists. It logs method, path and status for each request the Pingora proxy forwards, but no tenant, request id or duration.

    ```rust
            if let Some(err) = e {
                warn!(method, path, status, error = %err, "Proxy request failed");
            } else {
                info!(method, path, status, "Proxy request completed");
            }
    ```
  - There is no audit-log code, no log on upstream/route create/update/delete, no sampling, and no OAGW tracing spans. The only span handling is that the WebSocket bridge task inherits the current span ([`proxy.rs:242-254`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/handlers/proxy.rs#L242-L254)).
  - [`headers.rs:27-29`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/headers.rs#L27-L29): under the default passthrough mode `none` no inbound header is forwarded, so `traceparent`/`tracestate` never reach the upstream and the trace breaks.

    ```rust
        let mut out = match mode {
            PassthroughMode::None => HeaderMap::new(),
            PassthroughMode::All => inbound.clone(),
    ```
  - [`model.rs:134-136`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/model.rs#L134-L136): `None` is the default passthrough mode.
- **Evidence of intent:** planned work. `F0008` has 157 `[ ]` and 0 `[x]`. The completion log's comment ("Log request summary with timing") says timing was intended but it is not logged.
- **Why this decision:** the PRD states the audit trail as a current MUST, but it is unbuilt, so it gets a planned marker instead of a build. Changing the PRD this way needs product sign-off. Trace propagation and the completion-log fields are cheap and fixed now.
- **Impact:** operators get tenant, request id and duration in the completion log, and traces continue into upstream calls. Upstreams start receiving `traceparent`/`tracestate`. Not breaking for clients. Follow-ups:
  - FUTURE: a note on [`PRD:522-524`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L522-L524) “100% of proxy requests logged with correlation ID” and [`PRD:727`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L727) (needs product sign-off), [`F0008:177-199`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0008-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-observability.md#L177-L199) “Structured JSON log line emitted to stdout”, [`F0008:336-356`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0008-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-observability.md#L336-L356), [`F0008:453-454`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0008-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-observability.md#L453-L454) “Audit log entries include `trace_id` field for distributed tracing correlation”, [`DESIGN:909-926`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L909-L926), [`INDEX:447-457`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/INDEX.md#L447-L457) (23.1-23.3) “Sensitive data not logged … Config change operations are logged”.
  - FIX-CODE: always forward `traceparent`/`tracestate` (done through the safe header allowlist of [P-11](#p-11-with-the-default-passthrough-the-upstream-never-sees-the-clients-x-request-id-accept-or-trace-headers)); add tenant, request id and duration to the existing completion log.
- **Decided together with:** [P-11](#p-11-with-the-default-passthrough-the-upstream-never-sees-the-clients-x-request-id-accept-or-trace-headers), [PLG-05](#plg-05-guards-check-the-gateways-outbound-headers-after-passthrough-filtering-and-auth-not-the-headers-the-client-sent).

### O-05: `secret_ref` values appear in a warning log and in client error details

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE`, P2. Never log `secret_ref` values or put them in error details, per `F0008`.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`F0008:193`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0008-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-observability.md#L193)
    > Scrub secrets: never include API keys, tokens, credentials, or secret_ref values
  - [`F0008:346`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0008-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-observability.md#L346)
    > The system **MUST NOT** log request/response bodies, query parameters, non-allowlisted headers, API keys, tokens, credentials, or secret_ref values.
- **What the code does:**
  - [`bind.rs:180`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L180): when the credential store is unavailable, the raw ref goes into a warning log.

    ```rust
                tracing::warn!(secret_ref = raw_ref, error = %msg, "cred_store unavailable during secret_ref validation");
    ```
  - [`bind.rs:163`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L163) and [`bind.rs:172-174`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L172-L174): the raw ref is written into the error `detail`.

    ```rust
                detail: format!(
                    "secret_ref '{raw_ref}' is not accessible to this tenant \
    ```
  - The same text is also built at [`bind.rs:190-192`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L190-L192) and [`bind.rs:196-198`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L196-L198).
  - [`error.rs:237-240`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/error.rs#L237-L240): that `detail` is returned to the client as a precondition violation on `auth.config.secret_ref`.
- **Evidence of intent:** none.
- **Why this decision:** `F0008` says MUST NOT, and nothing argues for the current behaviour.
- **Impact:** the management API error text no longer echoes the ref; status and error type stay the same. Operators no longer see the ref in the warning log. Not breaking. Follow-up: fix [`bind.rs:163`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L163), [`bind.rs:172-174`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L172-L174) and [`bind.rs:180`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L180) (and the same text at [l.190-192](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L190-L192) and [l.196-198](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L196-L198)).

## 9. Scenario catalogue integrity

The scenario catalogue has swapped component roles in the flows, dangling INDEX pointers, ID collisions, broken links and almost no traceability from the e2e tests; all fixes are doc-only.

Mechanical doc fixes in this area: [C-01](#scenario-catalogue), [C-02](#scenario-catalogue), [C-03](#scenario-catalogue), [C-04](#scenario-catalogue), [C-05](#scenario-catalogue) (see [Mechanical doc fixes](#mechanical-doc-fixes)).

## 11. Gear configuration (OagwConfig) vs docs

This area compares the gear configuration that OAGW actually reads with what the docs say about it.

The configuration is `OagwConfig` in [`config.rs:8-68`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L8-L68). `OagwConfig` and every nested struct (`SsrfPolicy` in [`ssrf.rs:18-20`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/ssrf.rs#L18-L20), `MetricsConfig` in [`config.rs:71-73`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L71-L73)) reject unknown keys, as in [`config.rs:8-10`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L8-L10):

```rust
#[derive(Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
pub struct OagwConfig {
```

So **a documented key that doesn't exist makes gear init fail**; it is not silently ignored. The rewrite of the config reference (every field below, the wrong key names, the README) is decided under [A-05](#config-reference), a [mechanical doc fix](#mechanical-doc-fixes). The two decisions owned by this area, [CFG-HTTP](#cfg-http-the-mini-chat-helm-chart-turns-on-plaintext-http-upstreams-against-the-https-only-constraint) and [CFG-VAL](#cfg-val-oagw-starts-with-a-zero-proxy-timeout-body-limit-or-token-cache-size-instead-of-refusing-the-config), follow the reference tables.

### Field-by-field

| Key (code) | Default (code) | Where the docs mention it | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| `proxy_timeout_secs` | 30 ([`config.rs:117-119`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L117-L119)) | only [`README.md:52`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/README.md#L52) “proxy_timeout_secs = 30” | **Semantics undocumented.** One value sets both the data-plane wait for upstream headers ([`gear.rs:151`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/gear.rs#L151)) and Pingora's per-read `read_timeout` ([`gear.rs:113`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/gear.rs#L113), [`pingora_proxy.rs:643`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/pingora_proxy.rs#L643)), so it is also the effective SSE/WS silence limit ([STR-02](#str-02-sse-and-websocket-streams-are-cut-after-30-s-of-upstream-silence-not-after-the-300-s-idle-timeouts-likely)). Over REST, the api-gateway's hard-coded 30 s timeout layer ([`api-gateway/src/gear.rs:540-544`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/api-gateway/src/gear.rs#L540-L544)) caps every request whatever this value is. [`config/mini-chat.yaml:424`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/config/mini-chat.yaml#L424) and the mini-chat Helm chart ([`configmap.yaml:436`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/mini-chat/deploy/helm/mini-chat/templates/configmap.yaml#L436)) set 60, which takes effect only for in-process SDK callers. Not validated: `0` would time out every request ([CFG-VAL](#cfg-val-oagw-starts-with-a-zero-proxy-timeout-body-limit-or-token-cache-size-instead-of-refusing-the-config)). |
| `max_body_size_bytes` | 100 MiB ([`config.rs:121-123`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L121-L123)) | not by name. [`DESIGN.md:172`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L172) “Body size hard limit: 100MB.” and [`DESIGN.md:515`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L515) “Hard limit 100MB; reject before buffering” | The docs call it a *hard* limit, but it is configurable. Not validated (`0` rejects every body). Over REST the api-gateway body limit (16 MiB default, [`api-gateway/src/config.rs:15-17`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/api-gateway/src/config.rs#L15-L17)) answers first ([P-06](#p-06-an-oversized-body-gets-400-out_of_range-from-oagw-or-a-plain-text-413-from-the-api-gateway)). |
| `allow_http_upstream` | `false` ([`config.rs:15-22`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L15-L22)) | not by name | **Contradicts** the HTTPS-only constraint: [`DESIGN.md:174-176`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L174-L176) “plaintext upstreams explicitly blocked”, and [`F0007:350`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0007-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-streaming.md#L350) “HTTPS-only constraint (`cpt-cf-oagw-constraint-https-only`) applies to all streaming upstream connections.” It also allows plaintext OAuth2 **token endpoints** ([`gear.rs:129-133`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/gear.rs#L129-L133)). Rejected at startup in FIPS builds ([`config.rs:162-169`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L162-L169)). Turned on in [`config/e2e-local.yaml:530`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/config/e2e-local.yaml#L530) “Only for testing - do not enable in production without proper security controls” and in the mini-chat Helm chart ([`configmap.yaml:437`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/mini-chat/deploy/helm/mini-chat/templates/configmap.yaml#L437)), which is decided under [CFG-HTTP](#cfg-http-the-mini-chat-helm-chart-turns-on-plaintext-http-upstreams-against-the-https-only-constraint). |
| `token_cache_ttl_secs` | 300 | [`ADR-0016:107`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0016-oauth2-client-credentials-auth-plugin.md#L107) “The actual TTL is `min(config_ttl, expires_in − 30s safety margin)`” | **Consistent.** The code applies the same rule ([`oauth2_client_cred_auth.rs:16`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/plugin/oauth2_client_cred_auth.rs#L16), [`oauth2_client_cred_auth.rs:219-223`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/plugin/oauth2_client_cred_auth.rs#L219-L223)). |
| `token_cache_capacity` | 10 000 | [`ADR-0016:108`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0016-oauth2-client-credentials-auth-plugin.md#L108) “Maximum entries in the token cache.” | **Consistent.** Not validated (`0` is accepted, [CFG-VAL](#cfg-val-oagw-starts-with-a-zero-proxy-timeout-body-limit-or-token-cache-size-instead-of-refusing-the-config)). |
| `websocket_idle_timeout_secs` | 300 | not by name. [`F0007:149`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0007-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-streaming.md#L149) “no bytes in either direction within configured timeout” | Undocumented key. In practice it is overridden by `proxy_timeout_secs` ([STR-02](#str-02-sse-and-websocket-streams-are-cut-after-30-s-of-upstream-silence-not-after-the-300-s-idle-timeouts-likely)). |
| `websocket_close_timeout_secs` | 5 | [`F0007:150`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0007-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-streaming.md#L150) “each announcement bounded independently by `websocket_close_timeout_secs`”, [`F0007:272`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0007-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-streaming.md#L272) “under its own `websocket_close_timeout_secs` budget”, [`F0007:359`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0007-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-streaming.md#L359) “`websocket_close_timeout_secs` … system … 5”, [scenario 14.5](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/scenarios/protocols/websocket/negative-14.5-stalled-caller-does-not-block-upstream-close.md#L20) “within `websocket_close_timeout_secs` of teardown starting” | **Consistent** (name, scope and default). |
| `streaming_idle_timeout_secs` | 300 | [`F0007:358`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0007-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-streaming.md#L358) “`streaming_idle_timeout_seconds` … upstream / route … Inherited from `timeout` guard plugin or system default” | **Name, scope and inheritance are all wrong.** The documented name fails `deny_unknown_fields`. The real key is system-level only and applies only to the SSE upstream direction. The timeout guard is catalog-only: [`DESIGN.md:282`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L282) “identifiers exist only for types-registry cataloging and cannot be bound via `plugins.items[].plugin_ref`”. This line is also false: [`F0007:362`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0007-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-streaming.md#L362) “All parameters are read from upstream/route configuration at connection time.” |
| `protocol_cache_ttl_secs` | 3600 | [`F0007:360`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0007-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-streaming.md#L360) “`protocol_version_cache_ttl_seconds` … system … 3600 (1 hour)” | **Name wrong** (it would fail init). The default matches, and `0` disables the cache, as the config comment says ([`pingora_proxy.rs:81-83`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/pingora_proxy.rs#L81-L83); test [`protocol_cache_disabled_when_ttl_zero`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/pingora_proxy.rs#L1472-L1480)). |
| `management_api_enabled` | `true` | only as future work: [`DESIGN.md:957`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L957) “(a) config flag to disable or make CRUD endpoints read-only” | **Exists** (added in [`ee808fd90`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/ee808fd90d8fc7c77afdbb86b2dee2bccd9172e0)). When `false`, the write routes are not registered ([`routes/mod.rs:34-36`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/routes/mod.rs#L34-L36)), so writes return 404/405 rather than 403. Undocumented. |
| `ssrf_policy.{enabled, extra_deny_cidrs, allow_cidrs, extra_deny_hostnames, allow_hostnames}` ([`ssrf.rs:20-37`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/ssrf.rs#L20-L37)) | enabled, built-in deny-list | not by name. The SSRF flow [`F0008:243-254`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0008-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-observability.md#L243-L254) describes the checks and names only “explicitly configured allowed_internal_segments”, which is not a real key | Undocumented. Allow lists take precedence over deny lists: [`ssrf.rs:15-16`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/ssrf.rs#L15-L16) “if an IP/hostname matches an allow … list it is permitted regardless of deny lists”. Disabled in [`config/e2e-local.yaml:532`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/config/e2e-local.yaml#L532) and [`config/mini-chat.yaml:426`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/config/mini-chat.yaml#L426). |
| `metrics.prefix` | `""` → `snake_case("oagw")` ([`config.rs:74-91`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L74-L91)) | not by name. The docs hard-code the `oagw_` prefix, e.g. [`F0008:156`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0008-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-observability.md#L156) “Increment `oagw_requests_in_flight{host}` gauge at request start” | Undocumented. Also missing from the custom `Debug` output ([`config.rs:223-248`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L223-L248)). |
