### Validation gaps

`OagwConfig::validate` ([`config.rs:152-171`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L152-L171)) runs at gear init and checks only the values below. The gaps are decided under [CFG-VAL](#cfg-val-oagw-starts-with-a-zero-proxy-timeout-body-limit-or-token-cache-size-instead-of-refusing-the-config).

| Key | Checked at startup | Value `0` today |
|---|---|---|
| `websocket_idle_timeout_secs`, `websocket_close_timeout_secs`, `streaming_idle_timeout_secs` | yes, must be > 0 ([`config.rs:153-161`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L153-L161)) | startup fails |
| `allow_http_upstream` | `true` is rejected under `--features fips` ([`config.rs:162-169`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L162-L169)) | n/a |
| `proxy_timeout_secs` | no | accepted; every request would time out |
| `max_body_size_bytes` | no | accepted; every body is rejected |
| `token_cache_capacity` | no | accepted |
| `protocol_cache_ttl_secs` | no; `0` is a valid value ([`validate_accepts_zero_protocol_cache_ttl`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L319-L326)) | the ALPN cache is off |

### CFG-HTTP: The mini-chat Helm chart turns on plaintext HTTP upstreams, against the HTTPS-only constraint

- **Decision:** `WONTFIX` (+ FIX-DOC), no priority. Keep `allow_http_upstream: true` in the mini-chat Helm chart as an accepted exception, and document it next to the HTTPS-only constraint.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`DESIGN.md:174-176`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L174-L176) (`cpt-cf-oagw-constraint-https-only`)
    > HTTPS-only for upstream connections in MVP. SSRF prevention; plaintext upstreams explicitly blocked.
  - [`F0007:350`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0007-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-streaming.md#L350)
    > HTTPS-only constraint (`cpt-cf-oagw-constraint-https-only`) applies to all streaming upstream connections.
- **What the code does:**
  - [`configmap.yaml:434-437`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/mini-chat/deploy/helm/mini-chat/templates/configmap.yaml#L434-L437): the mini-chat Helm chart, a deployment artifact, turns the flag on.

    ```yaml
    oagw:
      config:
        proxy_timeout_secs: 60
        allow_http_upstream: true
    ```
  - [`gear.rs:129-133`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/gear.rs#L129-L133): the same flag also lets the OAuth2 plugin call token endpoints over plaintext HTTP.

    ```rust
    let token_http_config = if cfg.allow_http_upstream {
        tracing::warn!("allow_http_upstream is enabled — HTTP token endpoints also allowed");
    ```
  - [`config.rs:15-22`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L15-L22): the default is `false`; [`config.rs:162-169`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L162-L169): FIPS builds refuse to start with it on.
  - The local mini-chat config ([`config/mini-chat.yaml:422-426`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/config/mini-chat.yaml#L422-L426)) does not set the flag; the mini-chat e2e setup injects it ([`conftest.py:197-203`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/suites/mini_chat/conftest.py#L197-L203)).
- **Evidence of intent:** the Helm line was added in [`756e89104`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/756e8910411ea169defc5e124e8cd0c9c050b0f4), a mini-chat vLLM provider commit whose message gives no reason for plaintext upstreams. Test [`validate_rejects_allow_http_upstream_under_fips`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L328-L342) asserts that FIPS builds reject the flag. Nothing documents the mini-chat exception.
- **Why this decision:** the highest authority is a DESIGN principle (`cpt-cf-oagw-constraint-https-only`); the PRD doesn't require HTTPS-only. The flag stays off by default and FIPS builds reject it; outside test configs, the mini-chat chart is the only place that turns it on, and that exception is accepted.
- **Impact:** nothing changes for clients or operators (not breaking). Follow-up (FIX-DOC): document the exception next to `cpt-cf-oagw-constraint-https-only` ([`DESIGN.md:174-176`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L174-L176)): why mini-chat needs plaintext upstreams, and that the flag also allows plaintext OAuth2 token endpoints ([`gear.rs:129-132`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/gear.rs#L129-L132)). The flag itself goes into the config reference under [A-05](#config-reference).
- **Decided together with:** [A-05](#config-reference) (which leaves this Helm-chart question to this item).

### CFG-VAL: OAGW starts with a zero proxy timeout, body limit or token-cache size instead of refusing the config

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE`, P2. Reject `proxy_timeout_secs: 0`, `max_body_size_bytes: 0` and `token_cache_capacity: 0` at startup, and document the rules.
- **What the docs say:**
  - No doc states a range for these keys, and no doc lists the `OagwConfig` fields at all: [`F0001:91`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0001-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-domain-foundation.md#L91)
    > Load `OagwConfig` from configuration file (fields defined in `cpt-cf-oagw-design-overview`)
  - [`ADR-0016:108`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0016-oauth2-client-credentials-auth-plugin.md#L108) documents `token_cache_capacity` with no lower bound:
    > | `token_cache_capacity` | 10,000 | Maximum entries in the token cache. |
- **What the code does:**
  - [`config.rs:152-161`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L152-L161): `validate` checks only the three streaming timeouts.

    ```rust
    pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<(), String> {
        if self.websocket_idle_timeout_secs == 0 {
    // …
        if self.streaming_idle_timeout_secs == 0 {
            return Err("streaming_idle_timeout_secs must be > 0".to_owned());
    ```
  - [`gear.rs:67-69`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/gear.rs#L67-L69): init calls `validate`, so a failed check stops startup.

    ```rust
    let cfg: OagwConfig = ctx.config_or_default()?;
    cfg.validate()
        .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!("invalid OAGW config: {e}"))?;
    ```
  - [`gear.rs:151-152`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/gear.rs#L151-L152): `proxy_timeout_secs` and `max_body_size_bytes` go to the data plane unchecked, so `0` would time out every request or reject every body.
- **Evidence of intent:** tests [`validate_rejects_zero_idle_timeout`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L274-L281), [`validate_rejects_zero_close_timeout`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L283-L290) and [`validate_rejects_zero_streaming_idle_timeout`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L310-L317) assert that `0` is rejected for the timeouts that are checked. [`validate_accepts_zero_protocol_cache_ttl`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L319-L326) asserts that `0` is valid where it means "off". No test covers `0` for the three unchecked keys.
- **Why this decision:** no document addresses these ranges, so the code's own rule decides. `validate` exists to catch "values that would cause broken runtime behaviour" ([`config.rs:150-151`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L150-L151)), and `0` breaks every request for the first two keys.
- **Impact:** operators notice: a config that sets any of the three keys to `0` now fails at startup with "invalid OAGW config" instead of starting. No config in the repo sets them to `0`. Not breaking for clients. Follow-ups: add the checks and tests in `OagwConfig::validate` ([`config.rs:152-171`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/config.rs#L152-L171)); document the rules in the config reference ([A-05](#config-reference)).
- **Decided together with:** [A-05](#config-reference) (which leaves config validation to this item).

## 12. docs/schemas vs the schemas OAGW produces

This area compares the JSON schemas in [`docs/schemas`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/tree/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas) with the upstream, route and plugin shapes OAGW actually produces and accepts.

**Decision for the whole area:** align `docs/schemas` with the wire contract, in the same PR as the rest of the doc alignment ([SCH-02](#sch-02-the-doc-schema-set-doesnt-match-the-registered-types-two-types-have-no-doc-schema-and-one-referenced-type-is-never-registered) … [SCH-07](#sch-07-the-sharing-fields-fragment-has-an-old-shape), through [M-08](#m-08-the-json-schemas-in-docsschemas-do-not-match-what-the-api-accepts-and-returns)). Shape (fields, types, enums, required flags, defaults) must match the DTOs and OpenAPI. Intended value constraints stay, marked "not yet enforced" until the code enforces them ([M-09](#m-09-documented-validation-rules-are-ticked-x-but-missing-or-different-in-the-code), [R-12](#r-12-rate-limit-values-are-never-validated-cost-0-turns-limiting-off)). The plugin schemas describe the registered built-ins and their config keys. Add `protocol.v1` and `proxy.v1` schemas and give every file a `$id`. Verify by validating real REST payloads. Then register the aligned files as the GTS type schemas ([SCH-01](#sch-01-types-registry-holds-empty-placeholder-schemas-for-the-oagw-types-so-it-checks-nothing-about-upstreams-routes-or-plugins)). The hand-maintained copies stay as they are ([SCH-08](#sch-08-the-upstream-and-route-shapes-are-maintained-by-hand-in-several-places), `WONTFIX`).

OAGW produces or consumes the upstream, route and plugin shapes in **four** places, and none of them uses `docs/schemas`:

1. **GTS types registered in types-registry:** [`type_catalog.rs:33-71`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/type_catalog.rs#L33-L71).
2. **OpenAPI** generated from the REST DTOs (utoipa): [`docs/api/api.json`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/docs/api/api.json), from [`dto.rs`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs). CI fails when the committed file is stale ([`api_contracts.yml:3-5`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/.github/workflows/api_contracts.yml#L3-L5)).
3. **The types-registry provisioning parser**, which reads upstream and route GTS instances at `post_init`, with its own serde structs: [`type_provisioning.rs:41-346`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/type_provisioning.rs#L41-L346).
4. **SDK models:** [`models.rs`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw-sdk/src/models.rs), which mirror the DTOs.

Nothing loads `docs/schemas` at runtime: there is no `include_str!` and no build step. The only link is prose in DESIGN, for example [`DESIGN.md:248-250`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L248-L250) “**Base type**: `gts.cf.core.oagw.upstream.v1~` … Full schema:”, followed by a link to the file ([`DESIGN.md:248-284`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L248-L284) covers all five). Since April 2026 the files have only been renamed and moved ([M-08](#m-08-the-json-schemas-in-docsschemas-do-not-match-what-the-api-accepts-and-returns)): the last content change was [`7fbf9b4ea`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/7fbf9b4eab58b226473dcdf2ce28872c21c75522), followed by [`85c393cc2`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/85c393cc26a860271f42b8ffe26b67f32643914a) (GTS vendor slug) and [`547cc4548`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/547cc4548a6d6e1da991f1b2d70a7ebb9ec419e5) (repository rename).

### SCH-01: types-registry holds empty placeholder schemas for the OAGW types, so it checks nothing about upstreams, routes or plugins

- **Decision:** `FIX-CODE`, P2. Once the doc schemas match the wire ([M-08](#m-08-the-json-schemas-in-docsschemas-do-not-match-what-the-api-accepts-and-returns)), register them as the GTS type schemas instead of the placeholders.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`DESIGN.md:248-250`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L248-L250) names each doc file as the schema of its GTS type (same pattern for route and the three plugin types up to l.284):
    > **Base type**: `gts.cf.core.oagw.upstream.v1~` … Full schema:
  - [`F0001:275-277`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0001-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-domain-foundation.md#L275-L277)
    > - [x] `p1` - **ID**: `cpt-cf-oagw-dod-domain-gts-provisioning` … The system **MUST** implement `type_provisioning.rs` that registers all OAGW GTS schemas (`upstream.v1~`, `route.v1~`, `auth_plugin.v1~`, `guard_plugin.v1~`, `transform_plugin.v1~`) and built-in plugin instances with `types_registry` during gear startup.
- **What the code does:**
  - [`type_catalog.rs:15-22`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/type_catalog.rs#L15-L22): each of the 7 types is registered with an ID, a description and `"type": "object"`, and nothing else.

    ```rust
    fn schema_entity(gts_id: &str, description: &str) -> Value {
        json!({
            "$id": gts_uri!(gts_id),
            "$schema": "http://json-schema.org/draft-07/schema#",
            "description": description,
            "type": "object"
    ```
  - So types-registry validates nothing about upstream, route or plugin instances. A malformed provisioned upstream or route shows up only when `post_init` parses it: [`type_provisioning.rs:732-741`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/type_provisioning.rs#L732-L741) returns a validation error.
  - *Likely*, one bad instance fails OAGW startup: `post_init` propagates the error with `?`, for the parse and for each create ([`gear.rs:258-299`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/gear.rs#L258-L299)), and the runtime turns a `post_init` error into a startup error ([`host_runtime.rs:650-658`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/libs/toolkit/src/runtime/host_runtime.rs#L650-L658)). [`gear.rs:258`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/gear.rs#L258):

    ```rust
    let upstreams = provisioning.list_upstreams().await?;
    ```
- **Evidence of intent:** the DoD above was ticked `[x]` by the [bulk checkbox sync](#why-the-docs-drifted) ([`2aed1705e`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/2aed1705ef781a87695c9e29f9b09838ccf419f5)). No test asserts the schema content: the catalog tests ([`type_catalog.rs:83-144`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/type_catalog.rs#L83-L144)) check only IDs and counts.
- **Why this decision:** the feature doc's DoD requires registering the OAGW schemas, and DESIGN names the doc files as those schemas. Registering placeholders meets the letter but validates nothing. The files can be used only after the wire alignment ([SCH-02](#sch-02-the-doc-schema-set-doesnt-match-the-registered-types-two-types-have-no-doc-schema-and-one-referenced-type-is-never-registered) … [SCH-07](#sch-07-the-sharing-fields-fragment-has-an-old-shape)).
- **Impact:** operators notice: provisioned upstream and route instances that don't match the aligned schemas will fail validation in types-registry, which checks each instance against its type schema on admission ([`unit.rs:689-694`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/types-registry/types-registry/src/domain/admission/unit.rs#L689-L694)). *Likely*, one bad instance already fails OAGW startup today, so the failure moves earlier rather than appearing for the first time. Not breaking for REST clients (REST bodies are checked by the DTOs). Follow-ups:
  - **Depends on** the schema alignment ([SCH-02](#sch-02-the-doc-schema-set-doesnt-match-the-registered-types-two-types-have-no-doc-schema-and-one-referenced-type-is-never-registered) … [SCH-07](#sch-07-the-sharing-fields-fragment-has-an-old-shape), [M-08](#m-08-the-json-schemas-in-docsschemas-do-not-match-what-the-api-accepts-and-returns)).
  - Change `schema_entity()` ([`type_catalog.rs:15-22`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/type_catalog.rs#L15-L22)), for example via `include_str!`.
  - Add a test that provisioned sample instances validate against the registered schemas.
  - Check the *Likely* "one bad instance fails startup" path ([`gear.rs:258-299`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/gear.rs#L258-L299)).
  - With CORS deferred ([PLG-13](#plg-13-route-level-cors-is-ignored-unless-sharing-inherit-and-a-descendants-own-private-or-enforce-cors-is-dropped)), the schemas mark `cors` as unsupported.
  - The single-source question is [SCH-08](#sch-08-the-upstream-and-route-shapes-are-maintained-by-hand-in-several-places) (`WONTFIX`).
- **Decided together with:** [M-08](#m-08-the-json-schemas-in-docsschemas-do-not-match-what-the-api-accepts-and-returns), [SCH-02](#sch-02-the-doc-schema-set-doesnt-match-the-registered-types-two-types-have-no-doc-schema-and-one-referenced-type-is-never-registered) … [SCH-07](#sch-07-the-sharing-fields-fragment-has-an-old-shape), [SCH-08](#sch-08-the-upstream-and-route-shapes-are-maintained-by-hand-in-several-places), [PLG-13](#plg-13-route-level-cors-is-ignored-unless-sharing-inherit-and-a-descendants-own-private-or-enforce-cors-is-dropped).

### SCH-02: The doc schema set doesn't match the registered types: two types have no doc schema, and one referenced type is never registered

- **Decision:** `FIX-DOC`, no priority. Align `docs/schemas` with the wire; for this item, add `protocol.v1` and `proxy.v1` schemas.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`auth_plugin.v1:14-21`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/auth_plugin.v1.schema.json#L14-L21) (the same in the guard and transform schemas) uses a `plugin_type.v1` type as the `type` enum:
    > "type": { "type": "string", "format": "gts-identifier", "enum": [ "gts.cf.core.oagw.plugin_type.v1~cf.core.oagw.builtin.v1",

    followed by the matching `starlark.v1` ID.
  - [`oagw_upstream_sharing_fields.fragment.json:1-5`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/oagw_upstream_sharing_fields.fragment.json#L1-L5) is not a schema (no `$schema`, `type` or `properties` at the top), only a bare map of fields:
    > { "auth": { "type": "object", "properties": {
- **What the code does:** [`type_catalog.rs:36-42`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/type_catalog.rs#L36-L42) registers 7 schemas:

  | GTS type | Registered by code | Doc schema file |
  |---|---|---|
  | `gts.cf.core.oagw.upstream.v1~` | ✓ (placeholder) | [`upstream.v1.schema.json`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L3) “"title": "OAGW Upstream Service",” |
  | `gts.cf.core.oagw.route.v1~` | ✓ (placeholder) | [`route.v1.schema.json`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/route.v1.schema.json#L3) “"title": "OAGW Route",” |
  | `gts.cf.core.oagw.auth_plugin.v1~` | ✓ (placeholder) | [`auth_plugin.v1.schema.json`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/auth_plugin.v1.schema.json#L3) “"title": "OAGW Auth Plugin",” |
  | `gts.cf.core.oagw.guard_plugin.v1~` | ✓ (placeholder) | [`guard_plugin.v1.schema.json`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/guard_plugin.v1.schema.json#L3) “"title": "OAGW Guard Plugin",” |
  | `gts.cf.core.oagw.transform_plugin.v1~` | ✓ (placeholder) | [`transform_plugin.v1.schema.json`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/transform_plugin.v1.schema.json#L3) “"title": "OAGW Transform Plugin",” |
  | `gts.cf.core.oagw.protocol.v1~` | ✓ (placeholder) | **none** |
  | `gts.cf.core.oagw.proxy.v1~` (permissions) | ✓ (placeholder) | **none** |
  | `gts.cf.core.oagw.plugin_type.v1~` (`builtin`/`starlark`) | **not registered** | used as the `type` enum in all 3 plugin schemas |
  | sharing-fields fragment | n/a | [`oagw_upstream_sharing_fields.fragment.json`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/oagw_upstream_sharing_fields.fragment.json): not a schema |
- **Evidence of intent:** none for the doc side; the files date from the [design-era docs](#why-the-docs-drifted) and have not been reconciled.
- **Why this decision:** schemas are a reference contract, so the docs must match what the code registers ([how decisions were made](#how-decisions-were-made)).
- **Impact:** not breaking; docs only. Follow-ups: done in the same PR as the rest of the doc alignment ([M-08](#m-08-the-json-schemas-in-docsschemas-do-not-match-what-the-api-accepts-and-returns)); add `protocol.v1`/`proxy.v1` and a `$id` on every file (with [SCH-03](#sch-03-the-doc-schemas-have-no-id-so-tooling-cant-tie-them-to-a-gts-type)); verify by validating real REST payloads. [SCH-01](#sch-01-types-registry-holds-empty-placeholder-schemas-for-the-oagw-types-so-it-checks-nothing-about-upstreams-routes-or-plugins) depends on this.
- **Decided together with:** [M-08](#m-08-the-json-schemas-in-docsschemas-do-not-match-what-the-api-accepts-and-returns), [SCH-01](#sch-01-types-registry-holds-empty-placeholder-schemas-for-the-oagw-types-so-it-checks-nothing-about-upstreams-routes-or-plugins), [SCH-03](#sch-03-the-doc-schemas-have-no-id-so-tooling-cant-tie-them-to-a-gts-type) … [SCH-07](#sch-07-the-sharing-fields-fragment-has-an-old-shape).

### SCH-03: The doc schemas have no `$id`, so tooling can't tie them to a GTS type

- **Decision:** `FIX-DOC`, no priority. Give every file in `docs/schemas` a `$id`, as part of the schema alignment.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`upstream.v1:1-4`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L1-L4): the file starts with `$schema` and `title`, with no `$id`. None of the 6 files has one.
    > "$schema": "http://json-schema.org/draft-07/schema#", "title": "OAGW Upstream Service",
- **What the code does:**
  - The registered schemas do carry a `$id` (`gts://…`), see [`type_catalog.rs:17`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/type_catalog.rs#L17).
  - The repo's GTS analyzer only counts a [`*.schema.json`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/tools/gts-analyze/README.md#L28) “JSON Schema file (`*.schema.json` with a `$id`)” as a type definition when it has a `$id`: [`tools/gts-analyze/README.md:38`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/tools/gts-analyze/README.md#L38) “documents with a `$id` of `gts://gts.<…>` or `gts.<…>` register as type definitions”.
  - Running it on the baseline tree (the release build of `tools/gts-analyze`, `gts-analyze gears/system/oagw`, default flags) reports "GTS Types defined here: 0" and "GTS Instances defined here: 0". Every OAGW GTS ID appears only as a doc reference, with sdk and main counts of 0 (for example `gts.cf.core.oagw.upstream.v1~`: 64 doc references, 0 elsewhere). The code builds its IDs with `gts_id!` constants and `json!` entities, which the tool doesn't detect, and the doc schemas aren't recognised as definitions.
- **Evidence of intent:** none.
- **Why this decision:** schemas are a reference contract; a `$id` is what ties each file to its GTS type, and [SCH-01](#sch-01-types-registry-holds-empty-placeholder-schemas-for-the-oagw-types-so-it-checks-nothing-about-upstreams-routes-or-plugins) needs that to register them.
- **Impact:** not breaking; docs only. Follow-up: the `$id` values must be the registered type IDs, so that [SCH-01](#sch-01-types-registry-holds-empty-placeholder-schemas-for-the-oagw-types-so-it-checks-nothing-about-upstreams-routes-or-plugins) can load the files.
- **Decided together with:** [M-08](#m-08-the-json-schemas-in-docsschemas-do-not-match-what-the-api-accepts-and-returns), [SCH-01](#sch-01-types-registry-holds-empty-placeholder-schemas-for-the-oagw-types-so-it-checks-nothing-about-upstreams-routes-or-plugins), [SCH-02](#sch-02-the-doc-schema-set-doesnt-match-the-registered-types-two-types-have-no-doc-schema-and-one-referenced-type-is-never-registered), [SCH-04](#sch-04-the-plugin-schemas-describe-plugin-definitions-with-starlark-that-dont-exist-the-registered-plugin-instances-dont-conform) … [SCH-07](#sch-07-the-sharing-fields-fragment-has-an-old-shape).

### SCH-04: The plugin schemas describe plugin definitions (with Starlark) that don't exist; the registered plugin instances don't conform

- **Decision:** `FIX-DOC`, no priority. Rewrite the plugin schemas to describe the registered built-in plugins and their config keys.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`auth_plugin.v1:33`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/auth_plugin.v1.schema.json#L33) (the guard schema is the same):
    > "required": [ "id", "type", "config_schema" ]
  - [`transform_plugin.v1:40`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/transform_plugin.v1.schema.json#L40) adds `phase`:
    > "required": [ "id", "type", "phase", "config_schema" ]
  - [`auth_plugin.v1:26-30`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/auth_plugin.v1.schema.json#L26-L30): a Starlark source reference.
    > "source_ref": { "type": "string", "format": "uri", "pattern": "^/api/oagw/v1/plugins/.+/source$", "description": "Derived from plugin id. Starlark source fetched via GET {source_ref}."
  - The `type` enum is `builtin` or `starlark` ([SCH-02](#sch-02-the-doc-schema-set-doesnt-match-the-registered-types-two-types-have-no-doc-schema-and-one-referenced-type-is-never-registered)).
- **What the code does:**
  - [`type_catalog.rs:46-69`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/type_catalog.rs#L46-L69): the 12 plugin instances have no `type`, `config_schema` or `phase`, so they would fail the doc schemas. Each instance is only an ID and a description, [`type_catalog.rs:25-30`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/type_catalog.rs#L25-L30):

    ```rust
    fn instance_entity(gts_id: &str, description: &str) -> Value {
        json!({
            "$id": gts_id,
            "description": description
        })
    ```
  - There is no Starlark ([M-02](#m-02-the-plugin-management-api-plugins-does-not-exist-but-the-docs-describe-it-and-tick-its-entity-and-repository)).
  - Built-in plugin config is a flat `map<string,string>` ([`dto.rs:253-258`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L253-L258)), not validated against any `config_schema` ([PLG-04](#other-doc-only-fixes-plugins)).
- **Evidence of intent:** plugin CRUD and Starlark are planned, not built ([M-02](#m-02-the-plugin-management-api-plugins-does-not-exist-but-the-docs-describe-it-and-tick-its-entity-and-repository)); typed plugin config is planned ([PLG-04](#other-doc-only-fixes-plugins)).
- **Why this decision:** schemas must match the wire; the planned plugin-definition model belongs to [M-02](#m-02-the-plugin-management-api-plugins-does-not-exist-but-the-docs-describe-it-and-tick-its-entity-and-repository) and [PLG-04](#other-doc-only-fixes-plugins), not to the reference schemas.
- **Impact:** not breaking; docs only. Follow-up: the plugin schemas must accept the registered instances, which matters once [SCH-01](#sch-01-types-registry-holds-empty-placeholder-schemas-for-the-oagw-types-so-it-checks-nothing-about-upstreams-routes-or-plugins) registers them.
- **Decided together with:** [M-08](#m-08-the-json-schemas-in-docsschemas-do-not-match-what-the-api-accepts-and-returns), [SCH-01](#sch-01-types-registry-holds-empty-placeholder-schemas-for-the-oagw-types-so-it-checks-nothing-about-upstreams-routes-or-plugins), [SCH-02](#sch-02-the-doc-schema-set-doesnt-match-the-registered-types-two-types-have-no-doc-schema-and-one-referenced-type-is-never-registered), [SCH-03](#sch-03-the-doc-schemas-have-no-id-so-tooling-cant-tie-them-to-a-gts-type), [SCH-05](#sch-05-upstreamv1schemajson-differs-from-the-upstream-shape-the-api-accepts-and-returns) … [SCH-07](#sch-07-the-sharing-fields-fragment-has-an-old-shape), [PLG-04](#other-doc-only-fixes-plugins).

### SCH-05: [`upstream.v1.schema.json`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json) differs from the upstream shape the API accepts and returns

- **Decision:** `FIX-DOC`, no priority. Align [`upstream.v1.schema.json`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json) with the wire (the OpenAPI `CreateUpstreamRequest`/`UpstreamResponse` and the provisioning `UpstreamPayload`); keep intended constraints marked "not yet enforced".
- **What the docs say and what the code does:** the table comes from a scripted diff (resolved `$ref`s, flattened, compared property sets, types, enums, required flags and defaults), checked by hand against the serde defaults.

  | Path | Doc schema | Produced (code) | Notes |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | `plugins.items[]` | string: GTS ID **or** bare UUID, [`upstream.v1:113-128`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L113-L128) “"oneOf": [ … "format": "gts-identifier", … "format": "uuid",” | object `{plugin_ref (required), config: map<string,string>}`, [`dto.rs:253-266`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L253-L266) | **Wire-breaking.** Doc-shaped payloads fail to deserialize. |
  | `id` | [`upstream.v1:6-10`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L6-L10) “"format": "uuid",” | GTS string `gts.cf.core.oagw.upstream.v1~<uuid>`, [`dto.rs:405`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L405) | |
  | `tenant_id` | absent, and the root forbids extra fields: [`upstream.v1:142`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L142) “"additionalProperties": false,” | required in the response ([`dto.rs:406`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L406)); *accepted as input* by provisioning ([`type_provisioning.rs:304-305`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/type_provisioning.rs#L304-L305)) | |
  | `rate_limit.budget.{mode,total,overcommit_ratio}` | absent: [`upstream.v1:204-206`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L204-L206) “"rate_limit": { "type": "object", "additionalProperties": false,” | present ([`dto.rs:177-185`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L177-L185)) | |
  | `rate_limit.response_headers` | absent | present, default `true` ([`dto.rs:139-140`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L139-L140)) | |
  | `rate_limit.burst.capacity` | optional: [`upstream.v1:240-243`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L240-L243) “Defaults to sustained.rate if not specified.” | **required** inside `burst` ([`dto.rs:172-175`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L172-L175)) | Omitting the whole `burst` does fall back to `sustained.rate` ([`rate_limit.rs:56-59`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/rate_limit.rs#L56-L59), test [`outcome_limit_falls_back_to_sustained_rate_without_burst`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/rate_limit.rs#L667-L675)), so the doc's intent holds only at the object level. |
  | `rate_limit.{sustained.rate, burst.capacity, cost}` | [`upstream.v1:223-226`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L223-L226) “"minimum": 1,” (the same for `capacity` and `cost`) | `u32`, OpenAPI minimum 0, no validation ([`dto.rs:137-138`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L137-L138), [`dto.rs:155-175`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L155-L175); [R-12](#r-12-rate-limit-values-are-never-validated-cost-0-turns-limiting-off)) | |
  | `server.endpoints[].scheme` | [`upstream.v1:39-43`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L39-L43) “"enum": [ "https", "wss", "wt", "grpc" ],” and required, [`upstream.v1:61`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L61) “"required": [ "scheme", "host" ]” | the enum adds **`http`**; optional, default `https` ([`dto.rs:30-48`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L30-L48)) | |
  | `server.endpoints[].port` | [`upstream.v1:53-58`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L53-L58) “"default": 443, "minimum": 1, "maximum": 65535” | `u16`, minimum 0; default 443 whatever the scheme ([`dto.rs:46-52`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L46-L52)) | |
  | `server.endpoints` | [`upstream.v1:33-35`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L33-L35) “"minItems": 1,” | no `minItems` in the OpenAPI shape ([`dto.rs:54-57`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L54-L57)); the write path rejects an empty list ([`validation.rs:34-38`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/validation.rs#L34-L38)) | |
  | `alias` | pattern `^[a-z0-9]([a-z0-9.:-]*[a-z0-9])?$` ([`upstream.v1:19`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L19)) | no pattern; `validate_alias` also allows `_` ([`alias.rs:18-24`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/alias.rs#L18-L24); [M-09](#m-09-documented-validation-rules-are-ticked-x-but-missing-or-different-in-the-code)) | |
  | `tags[]` | [`upstream.v1:22-27`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L22-L27) “"pattern": "^[a-z0-9_-]+$"” | no pattern, no validation ([`dto.rs:335-336`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L335-L336)) | |
  | `protocol` | enum of the http and gRPC protocol IDs, [`upstream.v1:68-75`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L68-L75) “"type": "string", "enum": [” | free string, never validated ([`dto.rs:322`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L322); [M-04](#m-04-the-upstream-protocol-field-accepts-any-string-and-is-never-used)) | |
  | `auth.type` | optional (no `required` list in `auth`), [`upstream.v1:77-80`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L77-L80) “"auth": { "type": "object", "properties": {” | **required** when `auth` is present ([`dto.rs:63-66`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L63-L66)) | |
  | `auth.config` | free-form object, [`upstream.v1:94-97`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L94-L97) “"config": { "type": "object", "description": "Authentication plugin configuration."” | `map<string,string>` ([`dto.rs:69-70`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L69-L70)) | |
  | `cors.enabled` | **required**, [`upstream.v1:312`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L312) “"required": [ "enabled" ],” | optional, default `false` ([`dto.rs:237-238`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L237-L238)) | |
  | `cors.allowed_methods` | [`upstream.v1:297`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L297) “"default": [ "GET", "POST" ],” | default **`[]`** ([`dto.rs:241-242`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L241-L242); [PLG-12](#plg-12-cors-without-allowed_methods-rejects-every-cross-origin-request-while-the-docs-default-it-to-get-and-post)) | **Behavioural.** Omitting it rejects all actual CORS requests. |
  | `additionalProperties: false` (root, server, endpoints, headers, rate_limit, cors) | strict, e.g. [`upstream.v1:142`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/upstream.v1.schema.json#L142) “"additionalProperties": false,” | the DTOs lack `deny_unknown_fields` ([`dto.rs:319-320`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L319-L320)), so unknown fields are silently dropped ([R-10](#r-10-strategy-queue-and-degrade-are-accepted-but-behave-as-reject-the-queue-block-is-dropped)/[R-11](#r-11-circuit-breaker-concurrency-limit-and-backpressure-fields-are-silently-accepted-and-ignored) rely on this) | |
  | Update (PUT) | no separate schema | `UpdateUpstreamRequest` requires `tags` and `enabled` ([`dto.rs:341-359`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L341-L359); [M-03](#m-03-a-put-that-omits-tags-or-enabled-or-priority-on-routes-fails-with-422-while-docs-promise-400-and-show-such-bodies)) | |

  **Defaults that match** (doc default = serde default): `enabled: true`, `scheme: https`, `port: 443`, `auth`/`plugins`/`rate_limit`/`cors` `sharing: private`, `headers.request.passthrough: none`, `rate_limit.algorithm: token_bucket`, `sustained.window: second`, `scope: tenant`, `strategy: reject`, `cost: 1`, `cors.expose_headers: []`, `cors.allow_credentials: false` ([`dto.rs:17-247`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L17-L247)). The OpenAPI output omits all of these defaults (utoipa doesn't emit serde defaults), so a client can learn them only from the code.
- **Evidence of intent:** the wire shape changed on purpose after the schemas were written, and the schemas were never updated: [`216a9ccdc`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/216a9ccdc6e20b3262c46058008e30577fcba203) (GTS IDs in the API), [`bdcf33e55`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/bdcf33e55b2d4d09ace35b613c7f4c1cb953b7cd) (plugin bindings), [`a9b28ca4a`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/a9b28ca4ad491a4f27d458d9f2c8addb04c54846) (budgets). OpenAPI is generated from the DTOs and checked in CI, and the e2e suite uses the real shape ([M-08](#m-08-the-json-schemas-in-docsschemas-do-not-match-what-the-api-accepts-and-returns)).
- **Why this decision:** schemas are a reference contract and must match the wire; the constraints the code does not enforce yet stay in the schema as intent, marked "not yet enforced" ([M-09](#m-09-documented-validation-rules-are-ticked-x-but-missing-or-different-in-the-code), [R-12](#r-12-rate-limit-values-are-never-validated-cost-0-turns-limiting-off)).
- **Impact:** not breaking; docs only. Follow-ups: when [M-09](#m-09-documented-validation-rules-are-ticked-x-but-missing-or-different-in-the-code) and [R-12](#r-12-rate-limit-values-are-never-validated-cost-0-turns-limiting-off) add the checks, the schema marks those constraints as enforced; keep `allowed_methods.default` as `["GET","POST"]` for when CORS returns ([PLG-12](#plg-12-cors-without-allowed_methods-rejects-every-cross-origin-request-while-the-docs-default-it-to-get-and-post)), and mark `cors` as not supported ([PLG-13](#plg-13-route-level-cors-is-ignored-unless-sharing-inherit-and-a-descendants-own-private-or-enforce-cors-is-dropped)).
- **Decided together with:** [M-08](#m-08-the-json-schemas-in-docsschemas-do-not-match-what-the-api-accepts-and-returns), [SCH-01](#sch-01-types-registry-holds-empty-placeholder-schemas-for-the-oagw-types-so-it-checks-nothing-about-upstreams-routes-or-plugins) … [SCH-04](#sch-04-the-plugin-schemas-describe-plugin-definitions-with-starlark-that-dont-exist-the-registered-plugin-instances-dont-conform), [SCH-06](#sch-06-routev1schemajson-differs-from-the-route-shape-the-api-accepts-and-returns), [SCH-07](#sch-07-the-sharing-fields-fragment-has-an-old-shape).

### SCH-06: [`route.v1.schema.json`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/route.v1.schema.json) differs from the route shape the API accepts and returns

- **Decision:** `FIX-DOC`, no priority. Align [`route.v1.schema.json`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/route.v1.schema.json) with the wire (the OpenAPI route DTOs and the provisioning `RoutePayload`); keep intended constraints marked "not yet enforced".
- **What the docs say and what the code does:**

  | Path | Doc schema | Produced (code) | Notes |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | `upstream_id` | [`route.v1:20-24`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/route.v1.schema.json#L20-L24) “"format": "uuid",” | GTS string. **REST requires** `gts.cf.core.oagw.upstream.v1~<uuid>` ([`handlers/route.rs:36`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/handlers/route.rs#L36)); **provisioning accepts both** a GTS ID and a bare UUID ([`type_provisioning.rs:661-663`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/type_provisioning.rs#L661-L663)). | The two input paths differ. |
  | `plugins.items[]` | string (GTS ID), [`route.v1:47-52`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/route.v1.schema.json#L47-L52) “"type": "string", "format": "gts-identifier"” | object `{plugin_ref, config}` ([`dto.rs:253-266`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L253-L266)) | Wire-breaking (as in [SCH-05](#sch-05-upstreamv1schemajson-differs-from-the-upstream-shape-the-api-accepts-and-returns)). |
  | `cors` | absent: the root properties end with `rate_limit`, [`route.v1:58-63`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/route.v1.schema.json#L58-L63) “"rate_limit": { "$ref": "#/definitions/rate_limit",” (a `cors` definition exists at l.181-239 but no property uses it) | present ([`dto.rs:374-375`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L374-L375)), and ignored unless `inherit` ([PLG-13](#plg-13-route-level-cors-is-ignored-unless-sharing-inherit-and-a-descendants-own-private-or-enforce-cors-is-dropped)) | |
  | `priority`, `enabled` | absent | present: `priority: i32` default 0, `enabled` default true ([`dto.rs:378-381`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L378-L381)) | |
  | `rate_limit.budget`, `rate_limit.response_headers` | absent: [`route.v1:117-119`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/route.v1.schema.json#L117-L119) “"rate_limit": { "type": "object", "additionalProperties": false,” | present (shared `RateLimitConfig`, [`dto.rs:122-141`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L122-L141)) | |
  | `id` | [`route.v1:6-10`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/route.v1.schema.json#L6-L10) “"format": "uuid",” | GTS string ([`dto.rs:427`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L427)) | |
  | `tenant_id` | absent | present in the response ([`dto.rs:428`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L428)) and in provisioning input ([`type_provisioning.rs:329-330`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/type_provisioning.rs#L329-L330)) | |
  | `match.http.methods` | [`route.v1:70-76`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/route.v1.schema.json#L70-L76) “"minItems": 1, … "enum": [ "GET", "POST", "PUT", "DELETE", "PATCH" ]” | not enforced; same enum ([`dto.rs:275-281`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L275-L281)) | |
  | `match.http.path`, `grpc.service`/`grpc.method` | [`route.v1:79-81`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/route.v1.schema.json#L79-L81) “"minLength": 1,” (the same at l.104-112) | not enforced ([M-09](#m-09-documented-validation-rules-are-ticked-x-but-missing-or-different-in-the-code)) | |
  | `match`: exactly one of `http` or `grpc` | [`route.v1:33-36`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/route.v1.schema.json#L33-L36) “"oneOf": [ { "required": [ "http" ] }, { "required": [ "grpc" ] } ]” | enforced at write time ([`validation.rs:11-23`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/validation.rs#L11-L23)) | consistent |
  | Update (PUT) | no separate schema | `UpdateRouteRequest` requires `tags`, `priority` and `enabled` ([`dto.rs:384-397`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L384-L397)) | |
- **Evidence of intent:** as for [SCH-05](#sch-05-upstreamv1schemajson-differs-from-the-upstream-shape-the-api-accepts-and-returns): [`216a9ccdc`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/216a9ccdc6e20b3262c46058008e30577fcba203) made the REST `upstream_id` a GTS ID and [`bdcf33e55`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/bdcf33e55b2d4d09ace35b613c7f4c1cb953b7cd) introduced plugin bindings, without schema updates.
- **Why this decision:** schemas are a reference contract and must match the wire.
- **Impact:** not breaking; docs only. The schema has to describe both `upstream_id` forms or say which input path accepts which. Follow-ups: mark `cors` as not supported ([PLG-13](#plg-13-route-level-cors-is-ignored-unless-sharing-inherit-and-a-descendants-own-private-or-enforce-cors-is-dropped)); when [M-09](#m-09-documented-validation-rules-are-ticked-x-but-missing-or-different-in-the-code) adds the checks, mark those constraints as enforced.
- **Decided together with:** [M-08](#m-08-the-json-schemas-in-docsschemas-do-not-match-what-the-api-accepts-and-returns), [SCH-01](#sch-01-types-registry-holds-empty-placeholder-schemas-for-the-oagw-types-so-it-checks-nothing-about-upstreams-routes-or-plugins) … [SCH-05](#sch-05-upstreamv1schemajson-differs-from-the-upstream-shape-the-api-accepts-and-returns), [SCH-07](#sch-07-the-sharing-fields-fragment-has-an-old-shape).

### SCH-07: The sharing-fields fragment has an old shape

- **Decision:** `FIX-DOC`, no priority. Update [`oagw_upstream_sharing_fields.fragment.json`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/oagw_upstream_sharing_fields.fragment.json) to the current wire shape, as part of the schema alignment.
- **What the docs say:**
  - [`fragment.json:14-24`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/oagw_upstream_sharing_fields.fragment.json#L14-L24): a flat `rate_limit.{rate, window}`.
    > "rate": { "type": "integer" }, "window": { "type": "string" }
  - [`fragment.json:34-37`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/oagw_upstream_sharing_fields.fragment.json#L34-L37): plugin `items` as strings.
    > "items": { "type": "array", "items": { "type": "string", "format": "gts-identifier" }
  - There is no `cors` entry.
- **What the code does:** [`dto.rs:155-160`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L155-L160): the rate lives under `sustained.{rate, window}`; plugin items are `{plugin_ref, config}` objects ([`dto.rs:253-258`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs#L253-L258)).
- **Evidence of intent:** the fragment's content has not changed since [`3010e054f`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/3010e054fead23d9e60efa63fe7a2d4be11fd72c); a later commit only moved it.
- **Why this decision:** schemas are a reference contract and must match the wire ([M-08](#m-08-the-json-schemas-in-docsschemas-do-not-match-what-the-api-accepts-and-returns)).
- **Impact:** not breaking; docs only.
- **Decided together with:** [M-08](#m-08-the-json-schemas-in-docsschemas-do-not-match-what-the-api-accepts-and-returns), [SCH-01](#sch-01-types-registry-holds-empty-placeholder-schemas-for-the-oagw-types-so-it-checks-nothing-about-upstreams-routes-or-plugins) … [SCH-06](#sch-06-routev1schemajson-differs-from-the-route-shape-the-api-accepts-and-returns).

### SCH-08: The upstream and route shapes are maintained by hand in several places

- **Decision:** `WONTFIX`, no priority. Accept the hand-maintained copies (DTOs, provisioning mirrors, SDK models, `docs/schemas`) and rely on review.
- **What the docs say:** the docs don't say which copy is the source of truth; DESIGN points at `docs/schemas` ([`DESIGN.md:248-250`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L248-L250) “Full schema:”).
- **What the code does:**
  - The shapes are defined independently in [`dto.rs`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/dto.rs), in private serde mirrors in [`type_provisioning.rs:41-346`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/type_provisioning.rs#L41-L346) and in [`models.rs`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw-sdk/src/models.rs), plus the out-of-date `docs/schemas`.
  - They agree today except for `tenant_id` (accepted as provisioning input only) and `upstream_id` (the SDK takes a `Uuid`, [`models.rs:312-315`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw-sdk/src/models.rs#L312-L315); REST requires a GTS ID; provisioning accepts both). Nothing ties the copies together.
- **Evidence of intent:** none.
- **Why this decision:** the copies agree today apart from the two known differences above, so review is accepted as the control instead of tying them together.
- **Impact:** nothing changes (not breaking). Risk: the aligned `docs/schemas` ([M-08](#m-08-the-json-schemas-in-docsschemas-do-not-match-what-the-api-accepts-and-returns)), once registered as GTS schemas ([SCH-01](#sch-01-types-registry-holds-empty-placeholder-schemas-for-the-oagw-types-so-it-checks-nothing-about-upstreams-routes-or-plugins)), can drift silently from the DTOs.
- **Decided together with:** [SCH-01](#sch-01-types-registry-holds-empty-placeholder-schemas-for-the-oagw-types-so-it-checks-nothing-about-upstreams-routes-or-plugins), [M-08](#m-08-the-json-schemas-in-docsschemas-do-not-match-what-the-api-accepts-and-returns).

## Mechanical doc fixes

These items only bring docs in line with the code (or mark planned features as planned). They all go into the one doc PR. Each row has the decision, the evidence and the edit.

### Status markers (checkbox sync)

Checkboxes and phase markers that don't match the code. Untick what isn't built, tick what is.

| ID | Problem | Decision | Evidence and edits |
|---|---|---|---|
| O-06 | Feature checkboxes are wrong both ways: unbuilt steps are `[x]`, built ones are `[ ]`, a feature status line is unset and one feature's phase is wrong | `FIX-DOC`: one status-marker sync; a checkbox reflects the current code, including the persistence steps of [A-01](#a-01-the-docs-describe-database-persistence-but-all-config-lives-in-memory-and-restsdk-created-config-is-lost-on-restart). | Ticked by the [bulk checkbox sync](#why-the-docs-drifted) but not built, for example: [`F0004:196`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0004-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-proxy-engine.md#L196) “[x] - `p1` - DB: Check gRPC service and method from oagw_route_grpc_match”, [`F0004:216`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0004-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-proxy-engine.md#L216) “[x] - `p1` - DB: SELECT plugin FROM oagw_plugin WHERE id = :uuid”, [`F0004:449`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0004-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-proxy-engine.md#L449) “[x] Multi-endpoint upstream with common-suffix alias returns 400 MissingTargetHost”, [`F0002:505`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0002-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-management-api.md#L505) “[x] All operations require appropriate GTS permissions and return 403 on unauthorized access”, [`F0002:199`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0002-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-management-api.md#L199) “[x] - [`service.rs:1741-1760`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1741-L1760) rebuilds the response with the upstream status; [`handlers/proxy.rs:260-294`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/handlers/proxy.rs#L260-L294) copies status and headers, adds `x-oagw-error-source` and tags the response `ForeignPassthrough`, which [`canonical_error_layer.rs:117-119`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/libs/toolkit/src/api/canonical_error_layer.rs#L117-L119) returns untouched (without the tag it would re-wrap non-problem 4xx/5xx bodies; added in [`dc9519b3c`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/dc9519b3c7886be9f0716c09fdc4de045dbfe84b)).<br>Probe (throwaway test, not in the repo, so it can't be re-run): an upstream that reads the body and answers 400, 401, 403, 404, 409, 413, 422, 500, 502, 503 or 504 gave the same status, `ErrorSource::Upstream` and the original JSON body every time; `Retry-After`, `X-RateLimit-Remaining` and `WWW-Authenticate` were preserved, and so was a 413 after a 1 MiB body.<br>Edit: [`F0004:93`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0004-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-proxy-engine.md#L93) → as-is passthrough, per [`F0004:151`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0004-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-proxy-engine.md#L151) and [ADR-0013](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/ADR/0013-error-source-distinction.md#L60). |

| STR-03 | The docs promise 504 IdleTimeout when an SSE stream goes idle; the stream actually ends silently with a clean EOF | `FIX-DOC`: the SSE idle timeout ends the stream with a clean EOF (a 504 is impossible after a 200). | [`F0007:109`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0007-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-streaming.md#L109) “**RETURN** 504 IdleTimeout with `X-OAGW-Error-Source: gateway` - `inst-sse-12a`”<br>[`F0007:313`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0007-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-streaming.md#L313) “[x] Idle timeout during SSE streaming (no events within configured timeout) returns 504 IdleTimeout”<br>[`F0007:259`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0007-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-streaming.md#L259) (DoD) “idle timeout (504 IdleTimeout)”<br>Code: [`session_bridge.rs:538-541`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/session_bridge.rs#L538-L541) returns `None` (clean EOF) when the idle deadline fires; `DomainError::IdleTimeout` is never constructed outside tests. the request stream is dropped ([`service.rs:836-848`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L836-L848)). [`service.rs:1752`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1752) always returns `Body::Stream`, so `try_into_bytes()` returns `Err`. [`StreamingError::WebSocketConnect`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw-sdk/src/error.rs#L377) is never constructed.<br>Edit: [`api.rs:145-151`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw-sdk/src/api.rs#L145-L151), [`body.rs:20-26`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw-sdk/src/body.rs#L20-L26), [`usage.rs:10`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw-sdk/tests/usage.rs#L10): WS over the SDK is not consumable (101 + `Body::Empty`, crate-private handle); HTTP responses are always `Body::Stream`. Remove or document the never-constructed `StreamingError::WebSocketConnect`. |

## Corrections

These were found while verifying the quotes, snippets and line ranges against the baseline. The item text already uses the corrected facts and links. No decision, outcome or priority changed.

### References that are not what they seemed

- "PR #190" and "PR #1918" are not PRs of `constructorfabric/gears-rust`. The repo was created on 2026-05-22, and both numbers belong to its predecessor. The design proposal is cited through its commits ([`14ef3e26e`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/14ef3e26e3d0660cccbe8f7e8463d5fa3254c0e3), [`2078ff1da`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/2078ff1da266a327572515c50cd9a51e8dbf4744), [`141f08c64`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/141f08c649b048bdf4b4c9e7add110efb09054c6)). The control-plane refactor behind [R-16](#r-16-f0005-bind-and-permission-rules-differ-from-the-code) is cited as [`c4de73b22`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/c4de73b22add1528a7c73f99165f489b9548d7dc) and its merge commit [`320f0c595`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/commit/320f0c595c261eca81fe267a2720a7656fe52c6c).
- Notes that only flagged overlapping work in progress were dropped ([M-13](#m-13-changes-made-through-the-sdk-leave-the-load-balancer-and-rate-limit-state-stale), [M-16](#other-doc-only-fixes-management-api), [STR-13](#str-13-the-sdk-sse-parser-does-not-follow-the-w3c-rules-it-claims-to-follow), [SCH-05](#sch-05-upstreamv1schemajson-differs-from-the-upstream-shape-the-api-accepts-and-returns)). The decisions and all other facts in them are unchanged.
- [U-6](#u-6-tests-cover-only-a-get-500-passthrough) pointed to PLG-13 for the unused `/echo-401-once` mock route. The right item is [PLG-11](#other-doc-only-fixes-plugins).
- [STR-02](#str-02-sse-and-websocket-streams-are-cut-after-30-s-of-upstream-silence-not-after-the-300-s-idle-timeouts-likely) pointed to STR-05 for Problem-JSON injection. The right item is [STR-07](#str-07-an-abnormal-upstream-websocket-drop-writes-problem-json-bytes-into-the-open-tunnel-likely).

### Facts corrected

- [R-03](#r-03-under-an-inherit-ancestor-a-child-can-raise-its-own-limit-above-the-ancestors): the PRD does not conflict with the code. Its formula [`PRD.md:336`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/PRD.md#L336) caps a descendant only against an *enforced* ancestor. The "private parent caps an inherit child" evidence is [`merge.rs:264-266`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/merge.rs#L264-L266), not the bind test; that case now sits under [R-04](#r-04-an-ancestors-private-auth-rate-limit-and-plugins-reach-descendants-that-omit-the-field). The §10 row was changed to match.
- [O-04](#o-04-no-audit-log-config-change-log-or-log-sampling-and-the-trace-breaks-at-oagw): a basic per-request completion log does exist at [`pingora_proxy.rs:903-921`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/pingora_proxy.rs#L903-L921). It logs method, path and status, but no tenant, request id or duration.
- [PLG-01](#plg-01-six-cataloged-plugins-basic-bearer-timeout-cors-logging-metrics-have-no-implementation-but-some-docs-still-promise-them): "all 11 built-ins MUST exist" was a paraphrase. DECOMPOSITION only lists the 11 plugins; the MUST is in [`F0003:350`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0003-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-plugin-system.md#L350). Both are now quoted verbatim.
- [PLG-03](#plg-03-an-unknown-plugin-id-gives-401-auth-500-guard-or-is-silently-skipped-transform-not-the-documented-503): a `PluginNotFound` error already exists, but it maps to 404, and a unit test asserts that ([`error.rs:351-361`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/error.rs#L351-L361)). The proxy never raises it.
- [P-16](#p-16-there-is-no-per-route-timeout-and-oagw-sets-no-timeout-on-plugin-calls): "plugin calls have no timeout" is only partly true. OAGW sets none of its own, but OAuth2 token requests inherit toolkit-http's 30 s per-attempt timeout with up to 3 retries ([`toolkit-http config.rs:754-761`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/libs/toolkit-http/src/config.rs#L754-L761)).
- [P-04](#p-04-the-rest-handler-buffers-the-whole-request-body-before-checking-proxyinvoke): over REST, the api-gateway body limit (16 MiB by default, [`api-gateway config.rs:15-17`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/api-gateway/src/config.rs#L15-L17)) caps the body before OAGW's 100 MiB limit.
- [P-17](#other-doc-only-fixes-proxy): the api-gateway's 504 does carry a problem+json body. It still has no `X-OAGW-Error-Source`.
- [M-03](#m-03-a-put-that-omits-tags-or-enabled-or-priority-on-routes-fails-with-422-while-docs-promise-400-and-show-such-bodies): scenario 2.5's PUT body does send `enabled`; all seven listed scenario bodies omit `tags`. The 422 test is a POST test; PUT uses the same extractor, but no PUT-specific test exists.
- [M-10](#m-10-credential-references-are-resolved-at-write-time-for-secret_ref-only-and-the-docs-describe-a-different-check): the DESIGN credential-isolation principle ([`DESIGN.md:138`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/DESIGN.md#L138)) does not say "fail closed". That wording comes from a code comment ([`bind.rs:153-155`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/bind.rs#L153-L155)) and from F0005's acceptance criteria.
- [A-02](#a-02-the-documented-multi-layer-config-cache-l1l2redis-does-not-exist): F0008's cache steps and DoD are already `[ ]`. `OagwConfig` has OAuth2 token-cache and ALPN protocol-cache settings, but no config-cache settings.
- [A-04](#adr-statuses): ADR-0014 is also `proposed`; the original list left it out.
- [A-05](#config-reference): `websocket_close_timeout_secs` is documented ([`F0007:359`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0007-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-streaming.md#L359)), so it was dropped from the "undocumented fields" list. The README's outdated dependency and plugin lists are at [`README.md:12-16`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/README.md#L12-L16), not in the config block.
- [A-06](#a-06-docs-put-every-oagw-path-under-apioagwv1-but-the-gear-serves-oagwv1-and-error-instance-values-are-built-inconsistently): 289 lines under `docs/` and `scenarios/` contain `/api/oagw/v1` at the baseline, not 286.
- [U-1](#other-doc-only-fixes-proxy): the REST handler does not hard-code `x-oagw-error-source: upstream`. It writes the value from the response's error-source extension ([`handlers/proxy.rs:260-275`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/handlers/proxy.rs#L260-L275)).
- [U-2](#u-2-a-large-request-whose-upstream-answers-early-eg-413-before-reading-the-body-gets-a-gateway-503-instead-of-the-upstreams-response): on the SDK streaming path, a large body fails in the body task. That failure surfaces through the `abort_rx` branch of a biased select ([`service.rs:1117-1131`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/infra/proxy/service.rs#L1117-L1131)), not at the header write the original cited. The Content-Length precheck that rejects before reading the body is confirmed ([`handlers/proxy.rs:152-159`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/api/rest/handlers/proxy.rs#L152-L159)); the resulting connection reset is not.
- [C-02](#scenario-catalogue): 24 INDEX lines point to "no separate file", not 31.
- [C-05](#scenario-catalogue): the suite has 15 `test_*.py` files, and 14 of them cite no scenario IDs, not 18. The Docker-mode limitation is confirmed: the Docker mock runs the generic [`mock_server.py`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/testing/e2e/helpers/mock_server.py#L1-L3), which serves only `/ping`, `/health` and static files.
- [SCH-05](#sch-05-upstreamv1schemajson-differs-from-the-upstream-shape-the-api-accepts-and-returns): an empty `server.endpoints` list is rejected on write ([`validation.rs:34-38`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/oagw/src/domain/services/management/validation.rs#L34-L38)); only the OpenAPI shape lacks `minItems`.
- §11: the F0008 SSRF flow does name one config key, `allowed_internal_segments`, which does not exist. The mini-chat Helm chart does not disable SSRF; only the local [`config/mini-chat.yaml:426`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/config/mini-chat.yaml#L426) does.
- [PLG-13](#plg-13-route-level-cors-is-ignored-unless-sharing-inherit-and-a-descendants-own-private-or-enforce-cors-is-dropped), [M-08](#m-08-the-json-schemas-in-docsschemas-do-not-match-what-the-api-accepts-and-returns): the route schema has an unused `cors` definition ([`route.v1.schema.json:181-239`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/schemas/route.v1.schema.json#L181-L239)), but no `cors` property.
- §10 "Merge order (route vs tenant)": the claimed Side B source ("F0005 inst-merge-3 flow: route after") does not exist. F0005 consistently puts the route before the tenant chain; the nearest text is an order-independent `min()` at [`F0005:284-285`](https://github.com/constructorfabric/gears-rust/blob/66da80e5715e10c08c6fafd5e9832137389a3355/gears/system/oagw/docs/features/0005-cpt-cf-oagw-feature-tenant-hierarchy.md#L284-L285).
- Appendix: `e2e_http2_test` is a Rust test file, not a test function.
